{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"You are RED-PEN doing a RE-CHECK of fixes on SIGIL Phase 9 \"COMPANION\" (the phone companion + device-signed remote gesture arm). Repo /home/kali/sigil, branch `phase9-companion`, HEAD `57525d5`. A prior dual review found 4 BLOCK + 3 HIGH + 6 more; they've now been fixed across commits `83df1ba` (main fixes) and `57525d5` (anti-spam). Your job: (A) confirm each original exploit is now DEAD against the fixed code, and (B) hunt for NEW defects the fixes introduced. Read-only + you MAY run `~/.sigil/venv/bin/python` repro scripts (scratchpad only). The prior repros are in `/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/` (repro_nan.py, repro_selfverify.py, repro_toctou.py, repro_live.py, repro_verify_raise.py, etc.) — re-run them.\n\nRead `git show 83df1ba 57525d5` and the current: `sigil/spine/store.py` (the new spine_lock + flock + re-read-tip in append), `sigil/bridge/envelope.py` (verify_envelope guard, atomic consume, nonce sentinel), `sigil/gesture/session.py` (arm_by_device NaN guards + self-verifying record + _killswitch_engaged cache + pending_device_arm), `sigil/gesture/run.py` (device_arm wiring + attempt-once), `sigil/bridge/daemon.py` (submit_arm_request/submit_device_approval dedup + bind_ok CGNAT), `sigil/bridge/webapp/app.js` (arm button + outbox).\n\nVERIFY each fix is load-bearing (re-run the exploit, prove it's blocked):\n1. BLOCK-1 NaN: a signed `ts=NaN` or `ttl=NaN`/`+Inf` arm → REFUSED now (re-run repro_nan). Also probe OTHER non-finite/edge inputs: `ttl=\"120\"` (str), `ts` as a bool, a huge int ts, `ttl=1e400`, negative ttl, `nonce` as a float. Any that still bypass freshness or the clamp?\n2. BLOCK-2 self-verify: an arm with `ttl > MAX_DEVICE_TTL` → the recorded `gesture.session_armed` re-verifies against the device sig (re-run repro_selfverify). Confirm `effective_ttl` bounds the session and `ttl_seconds` is the original signed value.\n3. BLOCK-3 verify_envelope: an authorized pubkey + malformed short `sig` → clean `(False, reason)`, no crash, and over the live server (repro_live) → clean 401 not RemoteDisconnected.\n4. BLOCK-4/MED-4 nonce TOCTOU: 8 concurrent replays of ONE effectful envelope → exactly 1 accepted (re-run repro_toctou).\n5. HIGH-2 spine chain: 8 concurrent appends (fresh SpineStore per thread, like the server) → distinct seqs + verify() intact. THEN stress it harder: more threads, mixed writers (append + consume + submit_arm_request concurrently), and a WRITE concurrent with the dedup scan — does the chain ever fork, or a write get lost?\n6. HIGH-1 kill-switch cache: a panic is honored within the cache TTL; the arm path checks FRESH. Confirm a released kill-switch un-blocks within <=TTL.\n7. HIGH-3 wiring: run_gesture(device_arm=True) with a recorded fresh request ARMS; with a stale request it attempts <=once (no per-frame refusal spam).\n\nThen HUNT for NEW defects the fixes introduced — this is the priority:\n- **DEADLOCK / HANG on the new locks.** spine_lock is a re-entrant RLock + an flock inside append. Trace: consume() holds spine_lock then calls record_receipt→append (re-acquires RLock — OK re-entrant — then opens the file and takes flock LOCK_EX). submit_arm_request/submit_device_approval hold spine_lock, ITERATE all records (a read), then append (re-acquire + flock). Can two threads deadlock (lock-ordering inversion between the RLock and the flock)? Can the flock block forever if the same process already holds it on another fd? Is there any path that acquires spine_lock, then blocks on something that needs spine_lock held by another thread? RUN a concurrency stress (many threads doing submit + consume + append) and confirm it COMPLETES (no hang) AND stays correct. Also: does the flock break on a filesystem without flock (returns cleanly)? Does a long dedup scan under the lock stall all other writers (a self-inflicted latency/DoS)?\n- **Kill-switch cache staleness/correctness.** The 0.5s cache: after a panic, is there any sequence where handle() injects because the cache says not-engaged? After a release, does it stay falsely engaged forever (or only <=TTL)? Does the cache interact badly with a device-armed session whose owner panics — measure the actual worst-case injection-after-panic window.\n- **Dedup correctness.** submit_device_approval dedups via `iter_records(since_seq=(tgt or 0)-1)`. If tgt is None/0/negative, is the scan correct (does it miss a duplicate recorded before tgt, or scan wrong)? Can two DIFFERENT approvals (different target_seq, same device) collide or wrongly dedup? Can the dedup be defeated by a one-byte change to re-bloat? Does dedup under the lock + the append re-entrancy stay correct?\n- **pending_device_arm / attempt-once.** Can a request be consumed twice (arm, disarm within the same run_gesture, re-consume)? Does attempt-once wrongly skip a LEGITIMATE fresh request that arrived during a live session and should arm after it ends? Does pending_device_arm pick a forged/refused record as a candidate (it filters decision=='auto' + sig + pubkey — can a _refuse_arm record or another signal masquerade)?\n- **The armed record now carries `effective_ttl` + original `ttl_seconds`.** Does anything read `ttl_seconds` (original) as the enforcement value anywhere (making the clamp moot)? grep every reader of the armed record.\n- **nonce sentinel -1.** Any negative-nonce or int-overflow path that the `nonce < 0` check + sentinel misses?\n\nRun the full suite (`for t in tests/test_*.py; do ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python \"$t\" | tail -1; done`) and the Rust tests (`cd kernel && cargo test --release 2>&1 | grep \"test result\"`); report totals. Return: for each of 1–7 STATUS=HELD (with repro output) or BROKEN (new repro); plus any NEW defect with severity + file:line + repro + fix direction. If everything holds, give an explicit attestation of what you re-ran and why the fixes are sound and introduced nothing. Be adversarial — a working new bypass or a deadlock is worth far more than a hypothesis."},"uuid":"5791808f-26c7-43c3-801a-0dcd70b66354","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:25.278Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"5791808f-26c7-43c3-801a-0dcd70b66354","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"<command-message>crucible</command-message>\n<command-name>crucible</command-name>\n<skill-format>true</skill-format>"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main/.claude/skills/crucible\n\n# CRUCIBLE — operating the engine\n\nCRUCIBLE turns an authorized seed URL into **oracle-confirmed** findings, reasons over them,\nand produces auditable reports — inside a fail-closed safety stack. Its whole value is one\nrule; internalize it before anything else:\n\n> **A claim is a FACT only when a deterministic oracle fires over data a real target\n> produced.** You (the LLM) ADVISE where to look and what a result might mean; the oracle\n> CONFIRMS. Nothing else — not your confidence, not a critic's endorsement, not a plausible\n> story — promotes a claim to a fact.\n\nThe standing doctrine every reasoning call operates under is\n[framework/cognitive/metacognition.md](../../../framework/cognitive/metacognition.md) (it is\ninjected into every LLM system prompt). The full architecture — agents, event spine, veracity\nlayer, learning/metacognition core, safety stack — is\n[framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md](../../../framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md). The binding\nconstitution is [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md); never relax scope, destruction, evidence, or\nhonesty rules.\n\n## The invariants (never violate these)\n\n1. **Oracle authority.** Only a fired oracle confirms. Critics/RL/reflection/self-consistency\n   may only *advise, re-rank, defer, or abstain* — never promote a finding or override the oracle.\n2. **Coverage.** Never silently skip an authorized attack surface. Learning and reflection\n   *deprioritise* (re-rank / defer); they never gate a surface out.\n3. **Prove by re-execution.** A finding is a fact only if its retained `oracle_context`\n   re-fires. The veracity firewall (`framework/v2/veracity/`) re-runs proofs; it can only demote.\n4. **Non-circular learning.** An EXPLOITABLE label needs ≥2 independent corroborating oracle\n   kinds; a silent oracle is never auto-labelled a false positive.\n5. **Refuse honestly, fail closed.** Decline to conclude what you cannot ground; record every\n   refusal as evidence. Hard limits (scope, authorization, destruction, real user data) are\n   inviolable — when unsure, stop and ask.\n6. **Determinism + append-only.** No wallclock/global-rng in learning/reward math; the event\n   spine is append-only (supersede, never edit).\n\n## Structured workflows\n\n### Run an authorized engagement (end-to-end, gated)\n1. Confirm the active target's `targets/<slug>/charter.md` authorizes it (constitution §II).\n2. `python3 -m framework.v2 engage <slug> <seed-url> [--recon] [--spine]`\n   — crawls, audits, confirms via oracles, chains attack paths, scores confidence, and\n   (with `--spine`) mirrors the whole run onto the immutable blackboard event stream.\n   (`--strict-evidence` is a **`scan`**-only flag, below — not an `engage` flag.)\n3. Every request passes the full gate chain; an out-of-scope seed or tripped kill-switch\n   refuses *before* traffic and is recorded as a `refusal` event.\n\n### Loopback-only quick scan\n`python3 -m framework.v2 scan http://127.0.0.1:<port>/ --format json|sarif|html [--strict-evidence]`\n— the export states each finding's **live grounding** (fact / ungrounded / contradicted);\n`--strict-evidence` withholds non-fact findings from the rendered doc but keeps them in\n`--reverifiable-out`.\n\n### Re-verify a report (prove-don't-guess, offline)\n`python3 -m framework.v2 verify <report.json>` re-runs each finding's retained `oracle_context`.\n`python3 -m framework.v2 evidence ...` builds/verifies signed, hash-linked evidence certificates\n(and, with report claims bound in, fails closed when a report sentence has no backing evidence).\n\n### Inspect / audit the event spine\nThe blackboard (`framework/v2/agents/blackboard.py`) is the one append-only, typed, provenance-\nlinked event stream. Replay it with `Blackboard.replay(engagement=..., since_id=...)`.\nCryptographic tamper-evidence: `framework/v2/agents/spine_chain.py`\n(`build_spine_chain` / `sign_spine_head` / `verify_spine_head`).\n\n### Recon / intel, memory, self-improvement\n`python3 -m framework.v2 intel ...` (OSINT into the shared world-model; gated egress),\n`memory ...` (cross-engagement priors — never fabricate a score), `improve ...`\n(authorise-not-apply — it never self-applies a change).\n\n### Read-only operator console\n`python3 -m framework.v2 console` — loopback, read-only, zero-impact operator UI over the run.\n\n## What NOT to do\n- Do not build detection-evasion / identity-rotation / stay-hidden-from-defenders capability —\n  CRUCIBLE is correlatable and authorized by doctrine (constitution §VI).\n- Do not let any learned/critic/LLM signal enter the deterministic oracle/SCE/calibration inputs\n  or promote a finding.\n- Do not attack third parties (payment/IdP/CDN) — test only the operator's integration with them.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"5be2df89-08ee-4322-8fa8-bcf343a2d0c4","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:25.293Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"e5a9c616-7c55-4308-bfac-6a2106e7334f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01111X6SUPnpMnMAMo4NSzBD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\timport threading\n14\tfrom pathlib import Path\n15\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n18\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n19\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n20\t\n21\ttry:\n22\t    import fcntl  # POSIX advisory file lock — cross-PROCESS append serialization\n23\texcept ImportError:  # pragma: no cover — non-POSIX\n24\t    fcntl = None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n25\t\n26\t_LOCKS_GUARD = threading.Lock()\n27\t_LOCKS: dict[str, \"threading.RLock\"] = {}\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef spine_lock(path: Path | str) -> \"threading.RLock\":\n31\t    \"\"\"A process-wide RE-ENTRANT lock per resolved spine path. Serializes `append` (read-tip → write)\n32\t    across threads so concurrent writers can't fork the hash chain, and — being re-entrant — lets a\n33\t    caller make a check-then-append atomic (e.g. the nonce replay gate) while its inner `append` still\n34\t    acquires the same lock. Cross-PROCESS serialization is added by an flock inside `append`.\"\"\"\n35\t    key = str(Path(path).resolve())\n36\t    with _LOCKS_GUARD:\n37\t        lk = _LOCKS.get(key)\n38\t        if lk is None:\n39\t            lk = _LOCKS[key] = threading.RLock()\n40\t        return lk\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n44\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n45\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n46\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n47\t        end = f.tell()\n48\t        if end == 0:\n49\t            return None\n50\t        buf = b\"\"\n51\t        pos = end\n52\t        while pos > 0:\n53\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n54\t            pos -= step\n55\t            f.seek(pos)\n56\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n57\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n58\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n59\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n60\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n61\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tclass SpineStore:\n65\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n66\t        self.path = Path(path)\n67\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n68\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n69\t\n70\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n71\t    def append(\n72\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n73\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n74\t        ts: str | None = None,\n75\t    ) -> int:\n76\t        content = {\n77\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n78\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n79\t        }\n80\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n81\t        # Serialize the whole read-tip → write so concurrent writers (threaded bridge server, gesture\n82\t        # daemon) can't both fork off a stale tip and break the chain. Re-read the TRUE tip from disk\n83\t        # under the lock — `self._last` may be stale if another instance/process appended.\n84\t        with spine_lock(self.path):\n85\t            with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n86\t                if fcntl is not None:\n87\t                    try:\n88\t                        fcntl.flock(f.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)   # cross-process guard (advisory)\n89\t                    except OSError:  # pragma: no cover\n90\t                        pass\n91\t                last = self._read_last_entry()\n92\t                entry = append_entry([last], cert_digest) if last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n93\t                record = {\n94\t                    \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n95\t                    \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n96\t                }\n97\t                f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n98\t                f.flush()\n99\t            self._last = entry\n100\t        return entry.seq\n101\t\n102\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n103\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n104\t        if not self.path.exists():\n105\t            return\n106\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n107\t            for line in f:\n108\t                line = line.strip()\n109\t                if not line:\n110\t                    continue\n111\t                d = json.loads(line)\n112\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n113\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n114\t\n115\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n116\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n117\t            if r.seq == seq:\n118\t                return r\n119\t            if r.seq > seq:\n120\t                break\n121\t        return None\n122\t\n123\t    @property\n124\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n125\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n126\t\n127\t    def count(self) -> int:\n128\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n129\t\n130\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n131\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n132\t        return [\n133\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n134\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n135\t        ]\n136\t\n137\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n138\t        \"\"\"Two-layer UNKEYED integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n139\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link cleanly\n140\t        (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). This proves internal CONSISTENCY, not authenticity:\n141\t        a naive payload edit fails (1), and a mid-chain digest edit cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash\n142\t        break caught by (2) — BUT a writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash for the tip (no\n143\t        successor to cascade into) or forward-cascades a fork produces a self-consistent chain that\n144\t        passes here. Resistance to a recompute-capable writer is the owner-SIGNED head's job\n145\t        (`checkpoint.verify_checkpoint`, Ed25519 + monotonic last_seq). Use this for corruption/\n146\t        naive-tamper detection; use the signed head for tamper-EVIDENCE.\"\"\"\n147\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n148\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n149\t            content = {\n150\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n151\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n152\t            }\n153\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n154\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n155\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n156\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n157\t\n158\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n159\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n160\t        if not line:\n161\t            return None\n162\t        d = json.loads(line)\n163\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n164\t"}]},"uuid":"c246b69d-7589-4ad1-bf37-3ca92b740631","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:51.531Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e5a9c616-7c55-4308-bfac-6a2106e7334f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c246b69d-7589-4ad1-bf37-3ca92b740631","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAt6Abzej8PDo7mtZ7vh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MUazYfUTetCqhh5NnNWyBz","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/bridge/envelope.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2594,"cache_read_input_tokens":26938,"output_tokens":151,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2594},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":151,"cache_read_input_tokens":26938,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2594,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2594,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAt69fwBjSz8otRL5Q5E","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"3f5c3b09-e825-4946-acb6-c066510b2aa0","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:51.557Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"3f5c3b09-e825-4946-acb6-c066510b2aa0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MUazYfUTetCqhh5NnNWyBz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Device-signed, replay-resistant request envelope (Phase 9 W1-A) — the bridge auth keystone.\n2\t\n3\tThe phone (a device the owner AUTHORIZED via `sigil.mesh.authorize_device`) signs EACH request to the\n4\tdesktop bridge with ITS OWN Ed25519 key over the canonical envelope core. There is NO wire bearer\n5\tsecret: authentication IS the signature, verified against the owner-minted authorized-device set. This\n6\tmirrors the approval-record pattern (`agents.approvals._approval_message` / `verify_approval`) — same\n7\tcanonical-JSON message, same fail-closed `verify_one`, same trust pinned to owner-minted device keys\n8\t(a rogue/revoked device cannot self-authorize; a tampered field breaks the signature).\n9\t\n10\tReplay resistance is a deterministic, spine-anchored monotonic-nonce highwater PER DEVICE, enforced\n11\tATOMICALLY for EFFECTFUL requests: the check-then-receipt runs under the spine lock (re-entrant), so\n12\tconcurrent replays of one captured envelope can't both pass. An effectful request must carry a nonce\n13\tstrictly greater than the highest nonce that device has ever been receipted for. Reads are authenticated\n14\tby signature alone (the transport verifies them without a receipt and bounds their replay with a\n15\ttimestamp window). The receipt is an append-only spine event, so the watermark is tamper-evident memory.\n16\t\n17\tThis module is PURE by construction — NO wallclock, NO RNG. The caller supplies `nonce` and `ts` so the\n18\tJS phone client and Python reproduce the exact signed bytes (the parity contract) and so freshness is a\n19\tdeterministic function of the spine. A wallclock timestamp-WINDOW check (is `ts` recent?) belongs to\n20\tthe server transport layer, not here. Offense-free.\"\"\"\n21\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n22\t\n23\tfrom typing import Set, Tuple, Union\n24\t\n25\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sign, verify_one\n26\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n27\t\n28\t# The request vocabulary the bridge accepts — a fail-closed allow-list. `panic`/`relay` are effectful;\n29\t# the `read:*` family is side-effect-free. An action outside this set is refused even if validly signed.\n30\tACTIONS = frozenset({\"panic\", \"relay\", \"read:snapshot\", \"read:pending\", \"read:record\",\n31\t                     \"read:stream\", \"read:recall\"})\n32\t\n33\t# The spine signal marking a consumed request. Its records form the per-device nonce highwater.\n34\tRECEIPT_SIGNAL = \"mesh.request\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef envelope_message(core: dict) -> bytes:\n38\t    \"\"\"The exact bytes signed/verified — canonical JSON of the core. This is the JS<->Python parity\n39\t    contract: a future JS client MUST produce these identical bytes. Mirrors\n40\t    `agents.approvals._approval_message` (return bytes; encode only if `canonical_json` gave a str).\"\"\"\n41\t    m = canonical_json(core)\n42\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef build_core(device_pubkey: str, action: str, args: dict, nonce, ts) -> dict:\n46\t    \"\"\"The signed core. `nonce`/`ts` are caller-supplied (this module never reads a clock or RNG).\"\"\"\n47\t    return {\"v\": 1, \"device\": device_pubkey, \"action\": action, \"args\": args, \"nonce\": nonce, \"ts\": ts}\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef sign_envelope(device_key, core: dict) -> dict:\n51\t    \"\"\"Phone/test side: attach a DEVICE signature over the canonical core, producing the wire payload.\"\"\"\n52\t    return {**core, \"sig\": sign(device_key.private_key_b64, envelope_message(core))}\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef verify_envelope(payload, authorized: Set[str]) -> Tuple[bool, Union[dict, str]]:\n56\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed authentication. Rebuild the core (payload minus `sig`); refuse if there is no sig /\n57\t    no device / the device is not in the owner-minted authorized set; then Ed25519-verify the signature\n58\t    over the canonical core; finally require the (now-authenticated) action to be in the `ACTIONS`\n59\t    allow-list. Mirrors `agents.approvals.verify_approval`. Returns (True, core) or (False, reason).\"\"\"\n60\t    p = payload.payload if hasattr(payload, \"payload\") else payload\n61\t    sig = p.get(\"sig\")\n62\t    device = p.get(\"device\")\n63\t    if not sig or not device:\n64\t        return False, \"missing signature or device\"\n65\t    if device not in authorized:                       # trust pinned to owner-minted device keys\n66\t        return False, \"device is not authorized\"\n67\t    core = {k: v for k, v in p.items() if k != \"sig\"}\n68\t    try:                                               # verify_one RAISES on a malformed-length sig\n69\t        sig_ok = verify_one(device, envelope_message(core), sig)\n70\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — any crypto/decode error is a fail-closed refusal, never a crash\n71\t        return False, \"signature invalid\"\n72\t    if not sig_ok:\n73\t        return False, \"signature invalid\"\n74\t    if core.get(\"action\") not in ACTIONS:              # allow-list the authenticated action (fail-closed)\n75\t        return False, f\"unknown action: {core.get('action')!r}\"\n76\t    return True, core\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tdef record_receipt(store: SpineStore, core: dict) -> int:\n80\t    \"\"\"Append the append-only consumption receipt. Carries only the routing fields — never `args`\n81\t    (which may hold a subject) and never the signature; the record is auto-tier (an A0 event).\"\"\"\n82\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n83\t                        payload={\"signal\": RECEIPT_SIGNAL, \"device\": core[\"device\"],\n84\t                                 \"action\": core[\"action\"], \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"],\n85\t                                 \"ts\": core[\"ts\"], \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tdef device_nonce_highwater(store: SpineStore, device_pubkey: str) -> int:\n89\t    \"\"\"The highest receipted nonce for this device (-1 if none, so a first nonce of 0 is valid). Non-int\n90\t    nonces are tolerated (skipped).\"\"\"\n91\t    hi = -1\n92\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n93\t        p = r.payload\n94\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == RECEIPT_SIGNAL and p.get(\"device\") == device_pubkey:\n95\t            try:\n96\t                n = int(p.get(\"nonce\"))\n97\t            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n98\t                continue\n99\t            if n > hi:\n100\t                hi = n\n101\t    return hi\n102\t\n103\t\n104\tdef consume(store: SpineStore, payload, authorized: Set[str], *, effectful: bool) -> dict:\n105\t    \"\"\"Authenticate, gate replay for effectful requests, receipt, return the core. Raises `ValueError`\n106\t    with the refusal reason on any failure (fail-closed). An effectful request requires a nonce strictly\n107\t    fresher than this device's highwater; a read skips that gate but still receipts (advancing it).\"\"\"\n108\t    ok, core = verify_envelope(payload, authorized)\n109\t    if not ok:\n110\t        raise ValueError(core)\n111\t    try:\n112\t        nonce = int(core[\"nonce\"])\n113\t    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n114\t        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n115\t    if nonce < 0:\n116\t        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n117\t    if effectful:\n118\t        # ATOMIC check-then-receipt: hold the re-entrant spine lock across the highwater read AND the\n119\t        # receipt append, so two concurrent replays of one captured envelope can't both pass (TOCTOU).\n120\t        from ..spine.store import spine_lock\n121\t        with spine_lock(store.path):\n122\t            if nonce <= device_nonce_highwater(store, core[\"device\"]):\n123\t                raise ValueError(\"replay: nonce not fresh\")\n124\t            record_receipt(store, core)\n125\t    else:\n126\t        record_receipt(store, core)\n127\t    return core\n128\t"}]},"uuid":"40c2e13d-5af8-4cc6-9b90-97e3a72be9f8","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:51.580Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3f5c3b09-e825-4946-acb6-c066510b2aa0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c67265ee-ac23-4720-a63a-676906ee2156","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAt6UsLkmAWLkF87dtK3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JZg4m7JqTjYMJw45TuGCgy","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/gesture/run.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6290,"cache_read_input_tokens":29532,"output_tokens":150,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6290},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":150,"cache_read_input_tokens":29532,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6290,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6290,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAt6QGmSjn43EMC5YzYZ","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"2e626cc3-e81e-463a-b360-c5e9e98bf6a4","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:55.866Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c67265ee-ac23-4720-a63a-676906ee2156","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015mTHj5eySSjovKYy7CXYi2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SessionGate (Phase 8, WS-F, THE KEYSTONE) — the ONLY authority that injects input, and only inside\n2\tan owner-armed session. Two enforcement layers so a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended action:\n3\t\n4\t  Layer 1 — the SESSION: `arm()` REQUIRES the owner key (it cannot be armed without the owner\n5\t  identity) and appends a signed `gesture.session_armed` record — the tamper-evident AUDIT proof-of-\n6\t  authorization, indicator-lit. The INJECTION gate itself is the live in-memory session, held ONLY by\n7\t  the owner's own running daemon process; it is BOUNDED by a TTL and ended by disarm / hand-loss /\n8\t  expiry. Injection is REFUSED with no live session.\n9\t\n10\t  Layer 2 — per intent, the tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`hid.*` names): A1 (pointer\n11\t  move/click/scroll/drag) injects within the session; A2 (type/combo/app.launch) is QUEUED for a\n12\t  verified owner/device approval bound to `sha256(session_id|tool|args)` and NEVER auto-injected — so\n13\t  a gesture alone can never type a password or launch an app.\n14\t\n15\tOnly arm/disarm + DISCRETE actions (click/scroll/drag injected; type/launch queued) hit the spine —\n16\tper-frame pointer MOVES are telemetry (30 FPS records would DoS the append-only log).\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\timport math\n20\timport time\n21\timport uuid\n22\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n23\tfrom typing import Optional\n24\t\n25\t_KS_CACHE_TTL = 0.5    # bound the per-intent kill-switch consult to ≤2 spine scans/sec (never per-frame at 30fps)\n26\t\n27\tfrom ..agents.base import Tier\n28\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n29\tfrom .components import InputBackend\n30\tfrom .types import GestureIntent\n31\t\n32\tSESSION_ARMED = \"gesture.session_armed\"\n33\tSESSION_DISARMED = \"gesture.session_disarmed\"\n34\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"gesture.action\"\n35\tARM_REQUEST = \"gesture.arm_request\"\n36\t\n37\tMAX_DEVICE_TTL = 300.0        # a DEVICE-armed session is deliberately SHORTER than a local owner arm (1800)\n38\tARM_FRESHNESS = 30.0          # a device arm request must be recent (anti-stale / bounds the replay window)\n39\t_ARM_CORE = (\"signal\", \"device_id\", \"nonce\", \"ts\", \"ttl_seconds\")\n40\t\n41\tINTENT_TOOL = {\n42\t    \"move\": \"hid.pointer.move\", \"click\": \"hid.pointer.click\",\n43\t    \"scroll_left\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\", \"scroll_right\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\",\n44\t    \"drag\": \"hid.pointer.drag\", \"type\": \"hid.type\", \"combo\": \"hid.combo\", \"launch\": \"hid.app.launch\",\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef pending_device_arm(store, trusted_pubkey=None):\n49\t    \"\"\"The newest RECORDED device arm request (written by the bridge's `submit_arm_request`) that has NOT\n50\t    yet armed a session — a candidate for `arm_by_device`, which RE-VERIFIES it fully (auth / freshness /\n51\t    kill-switch / replay / single-session / TTL). Returns the request dict, or None. This is the wiring\n52\t    that makes the remote-arm path reachable end-to-end (the gesture daemon consumes it).\"\"\"\n53\t    armed = set()\n54\t    candidate = None\n55\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n56\t        p = r.payload\n57\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and p.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\":\n58\t            armed.add((p.get(\"device_pubkey\"), p.get(\"nonce\")))\n59\t        elif (p.get(\"signal\") == ARM_REQUEST and p.get(\"decision\") == \"auto\"\n60\t              and p.get(\"sig\") and p.get(\"pubkey\")):\n61\t            candidate = dict(p)                       # newest recorded request wins (seq order)\n62\t    if candidate is None or (candidate.get(\"pubkey\"), candidate.get(\"nonce\")) in armed:\n63\t        return None\n64\t    return candidate\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef arm_request_message(core: dict) -> bytes:\n68\t    \"\"\"The canonical bytes a device signs to request an arm — ONLY the signed core fields, so a caller\n69\t    cannot smuggle unsigned extras. Mirrors the approval/envelope canonical-json contract.\"\"\"\n70\t    from ..reuse import canonical_json\n71\t    m = canonical_json({k: core.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE})\n72\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode(\"utf-8\")\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tdef sign_arm_request(device_key, *, device_id: str, nonce, ts: float, ttl_seconds: float) -> dict:\n76\t    \"\"\"Phone-side: build a device-signed arm request. The device signs with its OWN key (never the owner\n77\t    trust-root); the owner authorized this device once via the mesh ledger. This is the credential the\n78\t    bridge records and `SessionGate.arm_by_device` verifies.\"\"\"\n79\t    from ..reuse import sign\n80\t    core = {\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"device_id\": device_id, \"nonce\": nonce, \"ts\": ts, \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds}\n81\t    return {**core, \"pubkey\": device_key.public_key_b64,\n82\t            \"sig\": sign(device_key.private_key_b64, arm_request_message(core))}\n83\t\n84\t\n85\t@dataclass\n86\tclass Session:\n87\t    session_id: str\n88\t    live: bool = True\n89\t    expires_at: float = 0.0            # wallclock deadline — a session is BOUNDED, never indefinite\n90\t\n91\t    def expired(self, now: float) -> bool:\n92\t        return self.expires_at > 0.0 and now >= self.expires_at\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tclass SessionGate:\n96\t    def __init__(self, store, backend: InputBackend, *, classifier=None, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n97\t        self.store = store\n98\t        self.backend = backend\n99\t        self._classifier = classifier\n100\t        self._owner_key = owner_key\n101\t        self._trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey\n102\t        self.session: Optional[Session] = None\n103\t        self._ks_engaged = False\n104\t        self._ks_checked_at = -1e9        # forces a fresh check on the first consult\n105\t\n106\t    def _trusted(self):\n107\t        if self._trusted_pubkey is None:\n108\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n109\t            self._trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()\n110\t        return self._trusted_pubkey\n111\t\n112\t    def _killswitch_engaged(self, now: float) -> bool:\n113\t        \"\"\"Kill-switch state, cached for ≤_KS_CACHE_TTL so a 30fps `handle` loop does not re-scan the\n114\t        append-only spine per frame (that scan is O(spine) — a self-DoS). Panic latency is bounded by\n115\t        the TTL. The arm path checks the kill-switch FRESH (uncached), so arming can never race a halt.\"\"\"\n116\t        if now - self._ks_checked_at >= _KS_CACHE_TTL:\n117\t            from ..governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n118\t            self._ks_engaged = KillSwitch(self.store).is_engaged()\n119\t            self._ks_checked_at = now\n120\t        return self._ks_engaged\n121\t\n122\t    def _cls(self):\n123\t        if self._classifier is None:\n124\t            from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n125\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n126\t        return self._classifier\n127\t\n128\t    # --- Layer 1: the owner-armed session ---------------------------------------------------------\n129\t    def arm(self, *, owner_key=None, ttl_seconds: float = 1800.0) -> Session:\n130\t        from ..governor.authn import signed_payload\n131\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair\n132\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else (self._owner_key or owner_keypair())\n133\t        if ok is None:\n134\t            raise RuntimeError(\"cannot arm a gesture session without the owner key (BLOCK-3)\")\n135\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n136\t        core = {\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid}\n137\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n138\t                          payload={**signed_payload(core, ok), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n139\t                                   \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds,\n140\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED (indicator lit — opt-in)\"})\n141\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=time.time() + ttl_seconds)\n142\t        return self.session\n143\t\n144\t    def disarm(self) -> None:\n145\t        if self.session is not None:\n146\t            self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n147\t                              payload={\"signal\": SESSION_DISARMED, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n148\t                                       \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"summary\": \"gesture session DISARMED\"})\n149\t            self.session.live = False\n150\t        self.session = None\n151\t\n152\t    # --- Layer 1b: DEVICE-signed remote arm (a deliberate, reviewed trust-widening) ----------------\n153\t    def arm_by_device(self, request: dict, *, now: Optional[float] = None) -> Optional[Session]:\n154\t        \"\"\"Arm on a VERIFIED, owner-authorized DEVICE signature (SIGIL §remote-arm). Every downstream\n155\t        bound is UNCHANGED — the A1-inject/A2-queue tier gate, the TTL, hand-loss/expiry disarm, and an\n156\t        owner disarm/kill that always wins. Fail-closed at each step; a refusal is recorded, never armed.\"\"\"\n157\t        from ..governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n158\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n159\t        from ..reuse import verify_one\n160\t        now = time.time() if now is None else now\n161\t        # (1) kill-switch FIRST — an owner halt (incl. a phone panic) beats any arm.\n162\t        if KillSwitch(self.store).is_engaged():\n163\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"kill-switch engaged\")\n164\t        # (2) owner-minted device key + valid signature over the signed core (RP-APPROVAL-2: the\n165\t        #     authorized set is owner-signed only, so a merely-presented key is never trusted).\n166\t        if request.get(\"signal\") != ARM_REQUEST:\n167\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"not an arm request\")\n168\t        pub = request.get(\"pubkey\")\n169\t        authorized = authorized_devices(self.store, self._trusted())\n170\t        core = {k: request.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE}\n171\t        try:                                            # verify_one RAISES on a malformed-length sig — treat as refusal\n172\t            sig_ok = bool(pub) and pub in authorized and verify_one(pub, arm_request_message(core), request.get(\"sig\", \"\"))\n173\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — any crypto/decoding error is a fail-closed refusal, never a crash\n174\t            sig_ok = False\n175\t        if not sig_ok:\n176\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"unauthorized or invalid device signature\")\n177\t        # (3) freshness — bounds the replay window and rejects a stale captured request. Written\n178\t        #     fail-CLOSED for non-finite input: `nan > 30` is False, so a signed NaN ts would slip a\n179\t        #     `>`-style gate; require finite AND within-window explicitly.\n180\t        try:\n181\t            ts = float(request.get(\"ts\"))\n182\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n183\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"missing/invalid timestamp\")\n184\t        if not math.isfinite(ts) or not (abs(now - ts) <= ARM_FRESHNESS):\n185\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"stale or non-finite timestamp\")\n186\t        # (4) replay — this (device, nonce) must not have armed before (the append-only spine is witness).\n187\t        nonce = request.get(\"nonce\")\n188\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n189\t            p = r.payload\n190\t            if (p.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and p.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\"\n191\t                    and p.get(\"device_pubkey\") == pub and p.get(\"nonce\") == nonce):\n192\t                return self._refuse_arm(request, \"replayed arm nonce\")\n193\t        # (5) single live session — a device arm NEVER displaces an existing/owner session.\n194\t        if self.session is not None and self.session.live and not self.session.expired(now):\n195\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"a gesture session is already live\")\n196\t        # (6) TTL clamp — shorter than a local owner arm; a deliberate trust-narrowing inside the widening.\n197\t        #     Reject non-finite/non-positive BEFORE the min() (min(nan,300)=nan → a never-expiring session).\n198\t        try:\n199\t            raw_ttl = float(request.get(\"ttl_seconds\", MAX_DEVICE_TTL))\n200\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n201\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"invalid ttl\")\n202\t        if not math.isfinite(raw_ttl) or raw_ttl <= 0:\n203\t            return self._refuse_arm(request, \"non-finite or non-positive ttl\")\n204\t        ttl = min(raw_ttl, MAX_DEVICE_TTL)\n205\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n206\t        # The record's AUTHORIZATION is the verified device signature — self-verifying vs the owner-signed\n207\t        # device ledger; actor=DEVICE mirrors submit_device_approval. Store the ORIGINAL SIGNED core\n208\t        # fields (so the sig re-verifies for ANY ttl, incl. a clamped one) + the effective TTL separately,\n209\t        # and ONLY known fields (never `**request` — no unsigned extras persisted).\n210\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n211\t                          payload={\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid, \"armed_by\": \"device\",\n212\t                                   \"device_id\": core[\"device_id\"], \"device_pubkey\": pub,\n213\t                                   \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"], \"ts\": core[\"ts\"],\n214\t                                   \"ttl_seconds\": core[\"ttl_seconds\"],   # ORIGINAL signed value → sig re-verifies\n215\t                                   \"effective_ttl\": ttl,                 # the CLAMPED ttl actually enforced\n216\t                                   \"sig\": request.get(\"sig\"), \"pubkey\": pub, \"authorization\": \"device-signed\",\n217\t                                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n218\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED by authorized device (remote)\"})\n219\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=now + ttl)\n220\t        return self.session\n221\t\n222\t    def _refuse_arm(self, request: dict, reason: str) -> None:\n223\t        self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n224\t                          payload={\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"tier\": \"A0\",\n225\t                                   \"device_id\": request.get(\"device_id\"), \"reason\": reason,\n226\t                                   \"summary\": f\"device gesture arm refused: {reason}\"})\n227\t        return None\n228\t\n229\t    # --- Layer 2: per-intent tier gate ------------------------------------------------------------\n230\t    def handle(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> dict:\n231\t        \"\"\"Authorize + act on ONE intent. Returns a verdict dict. Injection ONLY inside a live session\n232\t        and ONLY for A1 tools; A2+ queues for approval and injects NOTHING; a danger name → refused.\"\"\"\n233\t        if intent.kind == \"hand_lost\":\n234\t            self.disarm()\n235\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"hand lost — session disarmed\"}\n236\t        # An owner halt (incl. a phone panic_engage) neuters injection mid-session — critical for a\n237\t        # device-armed session the owner may not be watching. Cached (≤_KS_CACHE_TTL) so a 30fps loop\n238\t        # never re-scans the spine per frame; panic latency is bounded by the TTL.\n239\t        if self._killswitch_engaged(time.time()):\n240\t            self.disarm()\n241\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"kill-switch engaged — session disarmed\"}\n242\t        if self.session is None or not self.session.live:\n243\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"no armed session — injection refused\"}\n244\t        if self.session.expired(time.time()):             # BLOCK-2: a session is bounded, never indefinite\n245\t            self.disarm()\n246\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"session expired — disarmed\"}\n247\t        tool = INTENT_TOOL.get(intent.kind)\n248\t        if tool is None:\n249\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": f\"unknown intent {intent.kind}\"}\n250\t        tier = self._cls().classify(tool)                 # DERIVED, fail-closed A3 on any error\n251\t        args = f\"{intent.dx:.4f},{intent.dy:.4f},{intent.arg}\"\n252\t        if tier <= Tier.A1:\n253\t            self._inject(intent)\n254\t            # BLOCK-1: log the DISCRETE A1 injections that actually take effect (click/scroll/drag);\n255\t            # per-frame `move` is telemetry (logging it at 30 FPS would DoS the append-only spine).\n256\t            if intent.kind != \"move\":\n257\t                self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n258\t                                  payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n259\t                                           \"tool\": tool, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n260\t                                           \"summary\": f\"gesture {tool} injected\"})\n261\t            return {\"injected\": True, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool}\n262\t        # A2/A3 → QUEUE an approval bound to this exact action; inject NOTHING\n263\t        token = sha256_hex(f\"{self.session.session_id}|{tool}|{args}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n264\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"PERCEPTION\",\n265\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"queued\",\n266\t                                         \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n267\t                                         \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token, \"args\": args,\n268\t                                         \"subject\": f\"gesture action {tool} (awaiting approval)\"})\n269\t        return {\"injected\": False, \"queued\": seq, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token}\n270\t\n271\t    def _inject(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> None:\n272\t        b = self.backend\n273\t        if intent.kind == \"move\" or intent.kind == \"drag\":\n274\t            b.move(intent.dx, intent.dy)\n275\t        elif intent.kind == \"click\":\n276\t            b.click()\n277\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_left\":\n278\t            b.scroll(-1, 0)\n279\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_right\":\n280\t            b.scroll(1, 0)\n281\t"}]},"uuid":"a712114d-e120-47bf-a9ef-38f3281b97d6","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:55.870Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c67265ee-ac23-4720-a63a-676906ee2156","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"2e626cc3-e81e-463a-b360-c5e9e98bf6a4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JZg4m7JqTjYMJw45TuGCgy","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The live gesture-control daemon (Phase 8, WS-F F7) — camera stream → landmarks → classify → FSM →\n2\tSessionGate, mirroring `voice/run.py run_mic`. Arms the session on start (indicator lit), disarms in a\n3\t`finally` (indicator cleared even on crash). Graceful fallbacks; components are injectable so the\n4\twhole loop runs deterministically on scripted doubles with no camera/model/OS input.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom .pipeline import GesturePipeline\n10\tfrom .session import SessionGate\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef run_gesture(*, store=None, owner_key=None, source=None, landmarker=None, classifier=None,\n14\t                backend=None, pipeline=None, gate=None, max_frames: Optional[int] = None,\n15\t                auto_arm: bool = True, device_arm: bool = False, trusted_pubkey=None) -> int:\n16\t    \"\"\"Run the loop; returns the number of frames processed. Injectable for tests.\n17\t\n18\t    `auto_arm=True` (default) is the camera path: owner-arm the session locally on start. `auto_arm=False`\n19\t    is for a caller that passes an ALREADY-ARMED `gate` — e.g. a LOCAL owner-armed session consuming a\n20\t    `remote.RemoteLandmarkSource` (owner arms at the PC; the phone streams landmarks in).\n21\t\n22\t    `device_arm=True` (with `auto_arm=False`) is the REMOTE-arm path: while no session is live, each frame\n23\t    the loop consumes the newest RECORDED, not-yet-used device arm request (written by the bridge's\n24\t    `submit_arm_request`) via `SessionGate.arm_by_device`, which RE-VERIFIES it fully. Once armed, the\n25\t    phone's landmark stream drives the pointer, bounded exactly as a local arm (A1 inject / A2 queue,\n26\t    kill-switch/expiry/hand-loss disarm). This is the wiring that makes the trust-widening reachable.\"\"\"\n27\t    if store is None:\n28\t        from ..spine.store import SpineStore\n29\t        store = SpineStore()\n30\t    if source is None:\n31\t        from ..perception.camera_stream import CameraStreamSource\n32\t        source = CameraStreamSource()\n33\t    if landmarker is None:\n34\t        from .landmark import OnnxHandLandmarker\n35\t        landmarker = OnnxHandLandmarker()\n36\t    # STRICT fail-closed egress gate (Phase 9 W2-H): this loop injects HID off a live camera stream,\n37\t    # so it refuses UNLESS the landmarker is EXPLICITLY on-box (`egresses is False`) — stricter than\n38\t    # perceive's default-safe `getattr(..., \"egresses\", False)` form, because an unset/unknown flag\n39\t    # here would mean streaming owner PIXELS off-box while driving the pointer. This gate refuses a\n40\t    # model that would EGRESS owner imagery; a future `RemoteLandmarker` with `egresses=False` (an\n41\t    # authorized INBOUND landmark-DATA stream over the owner's own tunnel) is a DISTINCT,\n42\t    # separately-authorized thing — it egresses nothing and passes this gate.\n43\t    if getattr(landmarker, \"egresses\", True) is not False:\n44\t        store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n45\t                     payload={\"signal\": \"gesture.refused\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"tier\": \"A0\",\n46\t                              \"reason\": \"landmark model would egress owner imagery — the gesture \"\n47\t                                        \"loop is on-box only\"})\n48\t        return 0  # inject NOTHING; never armed\n49\t    if classifier is None:\n50\t        from .features import RuleClassifier\n51\t        classifier = RuleClassifier()\n52\t    if backend is None:\n53\t        from ..platform import input_backend\n54\t        backend = input_backend()\n55\t    pipe = pipeline or GesturePipeline()\n56\t    g = gate or SessionGate(store, backend, owner_key=owner_key, trusted_pubkey=trusted_pubkey)\n57\t    if auto_arm:\n58\t        g.arm(owner_key=owner_key)   # camera path: owner-arm locally. When False, the caller's gate is\n59\t        # already armed (a LOCAL owner-armed session) and we consume it without a second arm.\n60\t    n = 0\n61\t    _attempted: set = set()\n62\t    try:\n63\t        for frame in source.frames():\n64\t            if device_arm and (g.session is None or not g.session.live):\n65\t                from .session import pending_device_arm     # REMOTE-arm: consume a recorded arm request\n66\t                req = pending_device_arm(store, trusted_pubkey)\n67\t                if req is not None:\n68\t                    _key = (req.get(\"pubkey\"), req.get(\"nonce\"))\n69\t                    if _key not in _attempted:              # attempt each recorded request ONCE — a stale/\n70\t                        _attempted.add(_key)                # refused request must NOT re-spam a refusal per frame\n71\t                        g.arm_by_device(req)                # re-verifies fully; no-op if it doesn't pass\n72\t            reading = classifier.classify(landmarker.detect(frame))\n73\t            intent = pipe.on_frame(reading)\n74\t            if intent is not None:\n75\t                g.handle(intent)\n76\t            n += 1\n77\t            if max_frames is not None and n >= max_frames:\n78\t                break\n79\t    finally:\n80\t        g.disarm()\n81\t    return n\n82\t"}]},"uuid":"0691e836-4d35-483c-a0fe-91a651183f7b","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:55.888Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2e626cc3-e81e-463a-b360-c5e9e98bf6a4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"0de25026-4e8f-4602-91bb-b1b5e071bca7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Xuj2yjYUUH98PYq165yh9p","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"BridgeDaemon (Phase 7, WS-D D-v) — the desktop-side API the phone talks to over WireGuard. It\n2\treuses the owner-signed cores: the queue (`approvals.pending` with the authorized-device set), a\n3\tDEVICE-signed approval (accepted only while that device is authorized), a PANIC halt (any engage is\n4\tfail-safe), and a KERNEL command relay (`KernelDispatch` → the same WARDEN gate as voice/UI).\n5\t\n6\tThis module is the bridge LIBRARY CORE + API (the doctrine + crypto). The network TRANSPORT — an\n7\tHTTP server over WireGuard, reusing the WS-C two-plane server pattern bound to a `bind_ok` address —\n8\tis a documented NEXT slice, not shipped here. `bind_ok` is the bind predicate that transport MUST\n9\tuse: loopback or a PRIVATE (WireGuard) address only — NEVER 0.0.0.0 / a public address.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport ipaddress\n13\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n16\t\n17\t\n18\t_CGNAT4 = ipaddress.ip_network(\"100.64.0.0/10\")   # carrier-grade NAT — the range Tailscale assigns tailnet IPs\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef bind_ok(addr: str) -> bool:\n22\t    \"\"\"True iff `addr` is safe to bind: loopback, a PRIVATE (e.g. WireGuard) address, or the CGNAT range\n23\t    Tailscale uses — never 0.0.0.0 / a public / an unspecified address.\"\"\"\n24\t    try:\n25\t        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(addr)\n26\t    except ValueError:\n27\t        return False\n28\t    if ip.is_unspecified:                          # 0.0.0.0 / :: → refuse\n29\t        return False\n30\t    return ip.is_loopback or ip.is_private or (ip.version == 4 and ip in _CGNAT4)\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tclass BridgeDaemon:\n34\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n35\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n36\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n37\t        # the owner trust anchor — the persisted identity by default (injectable for tests)\n38\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n39\t\n40\t    def _authorized(self):\n41\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n42\t        return authorized_devices(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey)\n43\t\n44\t    def pending(self) -> List[dict]:\n45\t        \"\"\"The phone-facing queue — minimal fields only (no subject leaks over the tunnel).\"\"\"\n46\t        from ..agents.approvals import pending\n47\t        items = pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=self._authorized())\n48\t        return [{\"seq\": r.seq, \"tier\": r.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": r.kind} for r in items]\n49\t\n50\t    def recall(self, subject: str) -> Optional[dict]:\n51\t        \"\"\"Read-only remote RECALL — \"where did I last see X?\" — answered from the owner's own\n52\t        GROUNDED on-screen OCR history. Returns the perception-recall provenance dict VERBATIM\n53\t        (seq/entry_hash/when/frame_sha256/quote) or None: A0, read-only, no VLM and no paraphrase —\n54\t        the served `quote` is the owner's verbatim captured OCR line, never an advisory VLM lead.\n55\t\n56\t        This surfaces the owner's own on-screen text over the tunnel, so it leaks MORE than\n57\t        `pending()`'s minimal {seq,tier,kind} fields. The network SERVER (a later slice) MUST\n58\t        therefore gate this behind an authorized-device (device-signed) read request; this daemon\n59\t        method is only the read-only core.\"\"\"\n60\t        from ..perception.recall import recall\n61\t        return recall(self.store, subject)\n62\t\n63\t    def submit_device_approval(self, payload: dict) -> int:\n64\t        \"\"\"Append a DEVICE-signed approval the phone produced — ONLY if the signing device is\n65\t        currently authorized AND the signature verifies (fail-closed). A rogue/revoked device or a\n66\t        forged signature is refused; the signed target_seq binds (no replay).\"\"\"\n67\t        from ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n68\t        from ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n69\t\n70\t        class _Rec:\n71\t            def __init__(self, p): self.payload = p\n72\t        authorized = self._authorized()\n73\t        if payload.get(\"signal\") != APPROVAL_SIGNAL:\n74\t            raise ValueError(\"not an approval record\")\n75\t        if payload.get(\"pubkey\") not in authorized:\n76\t            raise ValueError(\"signing device is not authorized\")\n77\t        if not verify_approval(_Rec(payload), self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized):\n78\t            raise ValueError(\"approval signature invalid\")\n79\t        sig, tgt = payload.get(\"sig\"), payload.get(\"target_seq\")\n80\t        from ..spine.store import spine_lock\n81\t        with spine_lock(self.store.path):               # atomic dedup-then-append: a replayed body can't bloat\n82\t            for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=(tgt or 0) - 1):\n83\t                p = r.payload\n84\t                if p.get(\"signal\") == APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"pubkey\") == payload.get(\"pubkey\") and p.get(\"sig\") == sig:\n85\t                    return r.seq                        # already recorded — idempotent\n86\t            return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n87\t                                     payload={**payload, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n88\t                                     supersedes_id=tgt)\n89\t\n90\t    def submit_arm_request(self, request: dict) -> int:\n91\t        \"\"\"Record a DEVICE-signed gesture arm request (the transport layer) — ONLY if the signing device\n92\t        is authorized AND the signature verifies (fail-closed, mirrors submit_device_approval). The live\n93\t        SessionGate RE-VERIFIES and enforces freshness / kill-switch / single-session / TTL when it\n94\t        consumes this via `arm_by_device`, so recording is necessary-but-not-sufficient to arm.\"\"\"\n95\t        from ..gesture.session import ARM_REQUEST, _ARM_CORE, arm_request_message\n96\t        from ..reuse import verify_one\n97\t        authorized = self._authorized()\n98\t        if request.get(\"signal\") != ARM_REQUEST:\n99\t            raise ValueError(\"not an arm request\")\n100\t        pub = request.get(\"pubkey\")\n101\t        if pub not in authorized:\n102\t            raise ValueError(\"signing device is not authorized\")\n103\t        core = {k: request.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE}\n104\t        try:                                            # verify_one RAISES on a malformed-length sig — refuse cleanly\n105\t            ok = verify_one(pub, arm_request_message(core), request.get(\"sig\", \"\"))\n106\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n107\t            ok = False\n108\t        if not ok:\n109\t            raise ValueError(\"arm request signature invalid\")\n110\t        sig = request.get(\"sig\")\n111\t        from ..spine.store import spine_lock\n112\t        with spine_lock(self.store.path):               # atomic dedup-then-append: one captured body can't flood\n113\t            for r in self.store.iter_records():\n114\t                p = r.payload\n115\t                if p.get(\"signal\") == ARM_REQUEST and p.get(\"pubkey\") == pub and p.get(\"sig\") == sig:\n116\t                    return r.seq                        # already recorded — idempotent, no spine bloat\n117\t            # record ONLY the signed core + pubkey + sig (never `**request` — no unsigned extras persisted)\n118\t            return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n119\t                                     payload={\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"device_id\": core[\"device_id\"],\n120\t                                              \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"], \"ts\": core[\"ts\"],\n121\t                                              \"ttl_seconds\": core[\"ttl_seconds\"], \"pubkey\": pub, \"sig\": sig,\n122\t                                              \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n123\t\n124\t    def panic_engage(self, *, by: str = \"phone\") -> int:\n125\t        \"\"\"Halt the mesh from the phone. ANY engage halts (fail-safe) — no signature needed for the\n126\t        SAFE direction. Release stays owner-only at the desktop (the dangerous direction is signed).\"\"\"\n127\t        from ..governor.killswitch import SIGNAL as KS_SIGNAL\n128\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n129\t                                 payload={\"signal\": KS_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\", \"by\": by,\n130\t                                          \"reason\": \"panic halt from phone\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n131\t\n132\t    def relay(self, text: str) -> str:\n133\t        \"\"\"Relay a KERNEL command — the same T0-router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as voice/UI.\"\"\"\n134\t        from ..voice.dispatch import KernelDispatch\n135\t        return KernelDispatch().send(text)\n136\t"}]},"uuid":"83e07ff2-9e8a-4910-af77-e5f46f5bebc4","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:41:59.750Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0de25026-4e8f-4602-91bb-b1b5e071bca7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"046b401b-e5f3-44fd-b4b2-de1ab66e6eef","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E1dNzFd2kjhiiFLZxXeonv","type":"tool_result","content":"commit 57525d5c82ceb66e605874c99ebe45a5dd7370c2\nAuthor: Water Hacker <satoshinakamotobull@gmail.com>\nDate:   Sun Jul 19 01:40:16 2026 -0400\n\n    Phase 9: prevent per-frame refusal spam from a stale recorded arm request\n    \n    The remote-arm wiring re-polled pending_device_arm every frame; a stale/refused\n    recorded request never leaves the pending set, so it would record a refusal PER\n    FRAME (30/sec self-DoS). Fix: run_gesture attempts each recorded (pubkey,nonce)\n    at most once per invocation. +1 control (stale request -> <=1 refusal, never arms).\n    W2-K 18/18.\n    \n    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\n\ndiff --git a/sigil/gesture/run.py b/sigil/gesture/run.py\nindex 6615fdc..e3870dd 100644\n--- a/sigil/gesture/run.py\n+++ b/sigil/gesture/run.py\n@@ -58,13 +58,17 @@ def run_gesture(*, store=None, owner_key=None, source=None, landmarker=None, cla\n         g.arm(owner_key=owner_key)   # camera path: owner-arm locally. When False, the caller's gate is\n         # already armed (a LOCAL owner-armed session) and we consume it without a second arm.\n     n = 0\n+    _attempted: set = set()\n     try:\n         for frame in source.frames():\n             if device_arm and (g.session is None or not g.session.live):\n                 from .session import pending_device_arm     # REMOTE-arm: consume a recorded arm request\n                 req = pending_device_arm(store, trusted_pubkey)\n                 if req is not None:\n-                    g.arm_by_device(req)                    # re-verifies fully; no-op if it doesn't pass\n+                    _key = (req.get(\"pubkey\"), req.get(\"nonce\"))\n+                    if _key not in _attempted:              # attempt each recorded request ONCE — a stale/\n+                        _attempted.add(_key)                # refused request must NOT re-spam a refusal per frame\n+                        g.arm_by_device(req)                # re-verifies fully; no-op if it doesn't pass\n             reading = classifier.classify(landmarker.detect(frame))\n             intent = pipe.on_frame(reading)\n             if intent is not None:","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"aaca6360-7a5e-41b9-9b12-f10873ea76d3","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:42:16.108Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"046b401b-e5f3-44fd-b4b2-de1ab66e6eef","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

The sole change from the source is a\n6\tlocal `IntegrityError` in place of the framework's `EntitlementError` (decoupling).\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport base64\n11\timport binascii\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\t\n14\tfrom cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature\n15\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (\n16\t    Ed25519PrivateKey,\n17\t    Ed25519PublicKey,\n18\t)\n19\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.serialization import (\n20\t    Encoding,\n21\t    NoEncryption,\n22\t    PrivateFormat,\n23\t    PublicFormat,\n24\t)\n25\t\n26\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, Signature, TrustRoot\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass IntegrityError(Exception):\n30\t    \"\"\"Malformed key/signature material (a provisioning error, not an attacker path).\"\"\"\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _b64decode_exact(value: str, expected_len: int, what: str) -> bytes:\n34\t    try:\n35\t        raw = base64.b64decode(value, validate=True)\n36\t    except (ValueError, binascii.Error) as e:\n37\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} is not valid base64: {e}\") from e\n38\t    if len(raw) != expected_len:\n39\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} decodes to {len(raw)} bytes, expected {expected_len}\")\n40\t    return raw\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef load_public_key(public_key_b64: str) -> Ed25519PublicKey:\n44\t    return Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(_b64decode_exact(public_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 public key\"))\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef verify_one(public_key_b64: str, message: bytes, signature_b64: str) -> bool:\n48\t    \"\"\"True iff a valid Ed25519 signature; False on bad sig; raises only on malformed material.\"\"\"\n49\t    pub = load_public_key(public_key_b64)\n50\t    sig = _b64decode_exact(signature_b64, 64, \"Ed25519 signature\")\n51\t    try:\n52\t        pub.verify(sig, message)\n53\t        return True\n54\t    except InvalidSignature:\n55\t        return False\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n59\tclass ThresholdResult:\n60\t    satisfied: bool\n61\t    valid_signers: tuple[str, ...]\n62\t    threshold: int\n63\t    reason: str\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef verify_threshold(message: bytes, signatures: list[Signature], trust_root: TrustRoot) -> ThresholdResult:\n67\t    \"\"\"Count distinct trust-root authorisers with a valid signature; compare to threshold.\"\"\"\n68\t    by_id: dict[str, AuthorizerKey] = {a.key_id: a for a in trust_root.authorizers}\n69\t    valid: list[str] = []\n70\t    seen: set[str] = set()\n71\t    for sig in signatures:\n72\t        if sig.key_id in seen:\n73\t            continue\n74\t        seen.add(sig.key_id)\n75\t        authorizer = by_id.get(sig.key_id)\n76\t        if authorizer is None:\n77\t            continue\n78\t        if verify_one(authorizer.public_key_b64, message, sig.signature_b64):\n79\t            valid.append(sig.key_id)\n80\t    satisfied = len(valid) >= trust_root.threshold\n81\t    reason = (\n82\t        f\"{len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) >= threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n83\t        if satisfied else\n84\t        f\"only {len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) < threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n85\t    )\n86\t    return ThresholdResult(satisfied=satisfied, valid_signers=tuple(valid), threshold=trust_root.threshold, reason=reason)\n87\t\n88\t\n89\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n90\tclass KeyPair:\n91\t    public_key_b64: str\n92\t    private_key_b64: str\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tdef generate_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n96\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()\n97\t    raw_priv = priv.private_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PrivateFormat.Raw, NoEncryption())\n98\t    raw_pub = priv.public_key().public_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PublicFormat.Raw)\n99\t    return KeyPair(\n100\t        public_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_pub).decode(\"ascii\"),\n101\t        private_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_priv).decode(\"ascii\"),\n102\t Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(raw)\n108\t    return base64.b64encode(priv.sign(message)).decode(\"ascii\")\n109\t"}]},"uuid":"362553d2-91d7-46d0-9f23-682402772eb8","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:42:34.292Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cc621e4a-4cf8-48b8-8d4b-b09508d54864","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Only the imports are localised.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n11\tfrom .crypto import sign, verify_threshold\n12\tfrom .models import _GENESIS_PREV, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef _entry_hash(seq: int, prev_hash: str, cert_digest: str) -> str:\n16\t    return sha256_hex(canonical_json({\"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": prev_hash, \"seq\": seq}))\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef build_chain(cert_digests: list[str], *, start_seq: int = 0) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n20\t    entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n21\t    prev = _GENESIS_PREV\n22\t    for i, cd in enumerate(cert_digests):\n23\t        seq = start_seq + i\n24\t        eh = _entry_hash(seq, prev, cd)\n25\t        entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=seq, prev_hash=prev, cert_digest=cd, entry_hash=eh))\n26\t        prev = eh\n27\t    return entries\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef append_entry(entries: list[ChainEntry], cert_digest: str) -> ChainEntry:\n31\t    prev = entries[-1].entry_hash if entries else _GENESIS_PREV\n32\t    seq = (entries[-1].seq + 1) if entries else 0\n33\t    return ChainEntry(seq=seq, prev_hash=prev, cert_digest=cert_digest,\n34\t                      entry_hash=_entry_hash(seq, prev, cert_digest))\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef verify_chain(entries: list[ChainEntry]) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n38\t    prev = _GENESIS_PREV\n39\t    for i, e in enumerate(entries):\n40\t        if e.prev_hash != prev:\n41\t            return False, f\"chain break at seq {e.seq}: prev_hash mismatch (entry deleted/reordered)\"\n42\t        if e.entry_hash != _entry_hash(e.seq, e.prev_hash, e.cert_digest):\n43\t            return False, f\"chain break at seq {e.seq}: entry_hash mismatch (entry tampered)\"\n44\t        if i > 0 and e.seq != entries[i - 1].seq + 1:\n45\t            return False, f\"chain break: seq gap at {e.seq}\"\n46\t        prev = e.entry_hash\n47\t    return True, f\"{len(entries)} entr{'y' if len(entries) == 1 else 'ies'} link cleanly\"\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef _head_payload(head: SignedChainHead) -> dict:\n51\t    d = head.model_dump(mode=\"json\")\n52\t    d.pop(\"signatures\", None)\n53\t    return d\n54\t\n55\t\n56\tdef sign_head(entries: list[ChainEntry], *, engagement_slug: str, signers: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> SignedChainHead:\n57\t    head_hash = entries[-1].entry_hash if entries else _GENESIS_PREV\n58\t    last_seq = entries[-1].seq if entries else 0\n59\t    head = SignedChainHead(engagement_slug=engagement_slug, last_seq=last_seq,\n60\t                           entry_count=len(entries), head_hash=head_hash)\n61\t    msg = evidence_signing_bytes(_head_payload(head))\n62\t    sigs = [Signature(key_id=kid, signature_b64=sign(priv, msg)) for kid, priv in signers]\n63\t    return head.model_copy(update={\"signatures\": sigs})\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef verify_head(head: SignedChainHead, entries: list[ChainEntry], trust_root: TrustRoot,\n67\t                *, prev_highwater: int | None = None) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n68\t    ok_chain, reason = verify_chain(entries)\n69\t    if not ok_chain:\n70\t        return False, reason\n71\t    exp_hash = entries[-1].entry_hash if entries else _GENESIS_PREV\n72\t    exp_seq = entries[-1].seq if entries else 0\n73\t    if head.head_hash != exp_hash or head.last_seq != exp_seq or head.entry_count != len(entries):\n74\t        return False, \"head does not match the chain (log truncated or head rewritten)\"\n75\t    thr = verify_threshold(evidence_signing_bytes(_head_payload(head)), head.signatures, trust_root)\n76\t    if not thr.satisfied:\n77\t        return False, f\"head signature invalid: {thr.reason}\"\n78\t    if prev_highwater is not None and head.last_seq < prev_highwater:\n79\t        return False, f\"rollback rejected: head last_seq {head.last_seq} < accepted high-water {prev_highwater}\"\n80\t    return True, f\"chain of {len(entries)} entr{'y' if len(entries)==1 else 'ies'} anchored by a valid signed head\"\n81\t"}]},"uuid":"bb425160-ede2-488b-bf8a-22e7695ff1c3","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:42:34.557Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b6e37993-230f-4fa1-ac3f-57d891622d9f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
