{"parentUuid":"599bf521-3257-4965-9323-e440243555ba","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WdqDFgJTbi3u25q2eAYRQE","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Deterministic canonical bytes + digests for spine integrity.\n2\t\n3\tVENDORED VERBATIM from CRUCIBLE `framework/v2/evidence/canonical.py` (owner's own work).\n4\tCopied — not imported — so SIGIL owns its integrity primitives, fully decoupled from the\n5\toffensive engine's environment (sovereignty doctrine). Behaviour is byte-identical.\n6\t\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport hashlib\n10\timport json\n11\tfrom typing import Any, Final\n12\t\n13\t# Never change without a schema bump + migration (invalidates all existing signatures).\n14\t_EVIDENCE_DOMAIN: Final[bytes] = b\"crucible-evidence-v1\\x00\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef canonical_json(payload: Any) -> bytes:\n18\t    \"\"\"Deterministic UTF-8 JSON: sorted keys (recursive), compact separators.\"\"\"\n19\t    return json.dumps(\n20\t        payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), ensure_ascii=False,\n21\t    ).encode(\"utf-8\")\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tdef sha256_hex(data: bytes) -> str:\n25\t    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef digest_payload(payload: Any) -> str:\n29\t    \"\"\"sha256 of a JSON-serialisable payload in canonical form.\"\"\"\n30\t    return sha256_hex(canonical_json(payload))\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef evidence_signing_bytes(certificate_payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:\n34\t    \"\"\"The exact bytes signed/verified (canonical JSON under the domain tag).\"\"\"\n35\t    return _EVIDENCE_DOMAIN + canonical_json(certificate_payload)\n36\t"}]},"uuid":"08fe7869-0851-41f8-91dd-95c08f863432","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:42:41.050Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"599bf521-3257-4965-9323-e440243555ba","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"11f7a2f3-1167-4a10-b806-65db986f03b4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GNmssk3wKnWNTJxkTUffww","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom typing import Optional\n12\t\n13\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n14\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n15\t\n16\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n17\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass KillSwitch:\n21\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n22\t        self.store = store\n23\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n24\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n25\t\n26\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n27\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n28\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n29\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n30\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n31\t\n32\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n33\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n34\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n35\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n39\t        engaged = False\n40\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n41\t            p = r.payload\n42\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n43\t                continue\n44\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n45\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n46\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n47\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n48\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n49\t        return engaged\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"1dae3c17-080c-40e8-b2b7-30dba486b108","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:42:41.276Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"11f7a2f3-1167-4a10-b806-65db986f03b4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Authentication IS an Ed25519 signature over the canonical envelope\n3\tcore by an owner-AUTHORIZED device key; replay is a spine-anchored per-device monotonic-nonce highwater.\n4\tRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_bridge_envelope.py\"\"\"\n5\timport tempfile\n6\t\n7\tfrom sigil.bridge import (ACTIONS, RECEIPT_SIGNAL, build_core, consume,\n8\t                          device_nonce_highwater, envelope_message, record_receipt,\n9\t                          sign_envelope, verify_envelope)\n10\tfrom sigil.mesh import authorize_device, authorized_devices, revoke_device\n11\tfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\n12\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n13\t\n14\tOWNER = generate_keypair()\n15\tOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n16\t\n17\t\n18\tdef _store():\n19\t    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tdef _authorized_device(s):\n23\t    \"\"\"Authorize a fresh device against OWNER; return (device_key, authorized_set).\"\"\"\n24\t    device = generate_keypair()\n25\t    authorize_device(s, \"phone-1\", device.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n26\t    return device, authorized_devices(s, OP)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t# ---- parity contract: the exact signed bytes a future JS client MUST reproduce -------------------\n30\tdef test_envelope_message_fixed_vector_parity():\n31\t    # canonical_json = sorted keys, compact separators, UTF-8. For this fixed core the bytes are EXACTLY:\n32\t    #   {\"action\":\"read:snapshot\",\"args\":{},\"device\":\"DEVKEYB64\",\"nonce\":1,\"ts\":1700000000,\"v\":1}\n33\t    core = build_core(\"DEVKEYB64\", \"read:snapshot\", {}, 1, 1700000000)\n34\t    expected = b'{\"action\":\"read:snapshot\",\"args\":{},\"device\":\"DEVKEYB64\",\"nonce\":1,\"ts\":1700000000,\"v\":1}'\n35\t    assert envelope_message(core) == expected, \"the parity contract byte string must not drift\"\n36\t    assert isinstance(envelope_message(core), bytes), \"envelope_message returns bytes\"\n37\t\n38\t\n39\t# ---- a well-formed signed envelope from an authorized device verifies ----------------------------\n40\tdef test_well_formed_authorized_envelope_verifies():\n41\t    s = _store()\n42\t    device, authorized = _authorized_device(s)\n43\t    core = build_core(device.public_key_b64, \"read:snapshot\", {}, 1, 1700000000)\n44\t    payload = sign_envelope(device, core)\n45\t    ok, out = verify_envelope(payload, authorized)\n46\t    assert ok is True and out == core, \"an authorized, correctly-signed envelope verifies and returns the core\"\n47\t\n48\t\n49\t# ---- a foreign / unauthorized key is refused (fail-closed) ---------------------------------------\n50\tdef test_foreign_unauthorized_key_refused():\n51\t    s = _store()\n52\t    _authorized_device(s)                                       # some OTHER device is authorized\n53\t    authorized = authorized_devices(s, OP)\n54\t    foreign = generate_keypair()                               # this one is NOT authorized\n55\t    core = build_core(foreign.public_key_b64, \"read:snapshot\", {}, 1, 1700000000)\n56\t    payload = sign_envelope(foreign, core)\n57\t    ok, reason = verify_envelope(payload, authorized)\n58\t    assert ok is False and \"authorized\" in reason, \"a foreign key (validly self-signed) is not trusted\"\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef test_missing_signature_or_device_refused():\n62\t    s = _store()\n63\t    device, authorized = _authorized_device(s)\n64\t    core = build_core(device.public_key_b64, \"read:snapshot\", {}, 1, 1700000000)\n65\t    assert verify_envelope(core, authorized)[0] is False, \"no signature -> refused\"\n66\t    signed = sign_envelope(device, core)\n67\t    no_dev = {k: v for k, v in signed.items() if k != \"device\"}\n68\t    assert verify_envelope(no_dev, authorized)[0] is False, \"no device -> refused\"\n69\t\n70\t\n71\t# ---- replay resistance: monotonic nonce highwater on effectful requests --------------------------\n72\tdef test_consume_effectful_nonce_replay_resistance():\n73\t    s = _store()\n74\t    device, authorized = _authorized_device(s)\n75\t\n76\t    def env(action, nonce):\n77\t        return sign_envelope(device, build_core(device.public_key_b64, action, {}, nonce, 1700000000 + nonce))\n78\t\n79\t    # nonce=1 succeeds\n80\t    core1 = consume(s, env(\"relay\", 1), authorized, effectful=True)\n81\t    assert core1[\"nonce\"] == 1 and device_nonce_highwater(s, device.public_key_b64) == 1\n82\t\n83\t    # a SECOND nonce=1 (a replay) is refused\n84\t    try:\n85\t        consume(s, env(\"relay\", 1), authorized, effectful=True)\n86\t        assert False, \"a replayed effectful nonce must be refused\"\n87\t    except ValueError as e:\n88\t        assert \"replay\" in str(e), \"the refusal is a nonce-freshness replay error\"\n89\t\n90\t    # nonce=2 (fresh) succeeds\n91\t    core2 = consume(s, env(\"relay\", 2), authorized, 1700000000)\n122\t    payload = sign_envelope(device, core)\n123\t    ok, reason = verify_envelope(payload, authorized)\n124\t    assert ok is False and \"unknown action\" in reason, \"an action outside ACTIONS is refused\"\n125\t    assert \"read:everything\" not in ACTIONS\n126\t\n127\t\n128\t# ---- a revoked device is dropped from authorized -> verify + consume refused ----------------------\n129\tdef test_revoked_device_refused():\n130\t    s = _store()\n131\t    device = generate_keypair()\n132\t    authorize_device(s, \"phone-2\", device.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n133\t    assert device.public_key_b64 in authorized_devices(s, OP)\n134\t    revoke_device(s, \"phone-2\", device.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n135\t    authorized = authorized_devices(s, OP)\n136\t    assert device.public_key_b64 not in authorized, \"a revoked device is no longer authorized\"\n137\t    payload = sign_envelope(device, build_core(device.public_key_b64, \"panic\", {}, 1, 1700000000))\n138\t    assert verify_envelope(payload, authorized)[0] is False, \"a revoked device's envelope does not verify\"\n139\t    try:\n140\t        consume(s, payload, authorized, effectful=True)\n141\t        assert False, \"a revoked device's request must be refused\"\n142\t    except ValueError:\n143\t        pass\n144\t\n145\t\n146\t# ---- record_receipt writes the minimal, subject-free routing fields ------------------------------\n147\tdef test_receipt_carries_no_args_and_is_auto_tier():\n148\t    s = _store()\n149\t    device, _ = _authorized_device(s)\n150\t    core = build_core(device.public_key_b64, \"relay\", {\"subject\": \"TOP SECRET wire $1M\"}, 5, 1700000005)\n151\t    seq = record_receipt(s, core)\n152\t    rec = s.get(seq)\n153\t    assert rec.payload[\"signal\"] == RECEIPT_SIGNAL and rec.payload[\"tier\"] == \"A0\"\n154\t    assert rec.payload[\"decision\"] == \"auto\" and rec.payload[\"nonce\"] == 5\n155\t    assert \"args\" not in rec.payload and \"subject\" not in str(rec.payload), \"the receipt leaks no args/subject\"\n156\t\n157\t\n158\t# ---- purity: envelope.py reads no wallclock and no RNG -------------------------------------------\n159\tdef test_module_is_pure_no_clock_no_random():\n160\t    import inspect\n161\t\n162\t    import sigil.bridge.envelope as E\n163\t    src = inspect.getsource(E)\n164\t    for forbidden in (\"import random\", \"import time\", \"datetime\", \"now_iso\", \"time.time\", \"Date\"):\n165\t        assert forbidden not in src, f\"envelope.py must be pure: found {forbidden!r}\"\n166\t\n167\t\n168\tdef _authed():\n169\t    s = _store()\n170\t    dev = generate_keypair()\n171\t    authorize_device(s, \"dev\", dev.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n172\t    return s, dev, authorized_devices(s, OP)\n173\t\n174\t\n175\tdef test_malformed_signature_is_a_clean_refusal_not_a_crash():    # red-pen BLOCK-3\n176\t    s, dev, auth = _authed()\n177\t    bad = {**build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"read:pending\", {}, 1, 1700000000), \"sig\": \"AAAA\"}\n178\t    ok, _reason = verify_envelope(bad, auth)                       # verify_one would RAISE on a wrong-length sig\n179\t    assert ok is False, \"a malformed-length signature returns (False, reason), never an unhandled exception\"\n180\t\n181\t\n182\tdef test_nonce_zero_is_a_valid_first_value():                     # sweep LOW-7\n183\t    s, dev, auth = _authed()\n184\t    consume(s, sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"panic\", {}, 0, 1700000000)), auth, effectful=True)\n185\t    try:\n186\t        consume(s, sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"panic\", {}, 0, 1700000000)), auth, effectful=True)\n187\t        assert False, \"a replay of nonce 0 must be refused\"\n188\t    except ValueError:\n189\t        pass\n190\t\n191\t\n192\tdef test_concurrent_replay_of_one_effectful_envelope_accepted_once():   # red-pen BLOCK-4 / sweep MED-4\n193\t    import threading\n194\t    s, dev, auth = _authed()\n195\t    env = sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"relay\", {\"text\": \"x\"}, 5, 1700000000))\n196\t    results, barrier = [], threading.Barrier(8)\n197\t\n198\t    def worker():\n199\t        barrier.wait()\n200\t        try:\n201\t            consume(s, env, auth, effectful=True)\n202\t {results.count('ok')}\"\n212\t\n213\t\n214\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n215\t    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith(\"test_\")]\n216\t    passed = 0\n217\t    for fn in {"parentUuid":"c95e5f45-4f2b-49b5-a4b8-ec2590df96c0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019vbJEsnvRnqV1eHwsMmQJ7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL Phase 9 W2-K — device-signed REMOTE ARM of a gesture session (the deliberate, reviewed\n2\ttrust-widening). Proves the full threat table: only an owner-authorized device can arm; forged /\n3\treplayed / stale / after-revoke arms are refused; the TTL is clamped shorter than a local arm; a device\n4\tarm never displaces a live session; the owner's kill-switch/disarm always wins; and every downstream\n5\tbound (A1-inject / A2-queue) is UNCHANGED for a device-armed session.\n6\tRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_gesture_arm.py\"\"\"\n7\timport tempfile\n8\timport time\n9\t\n10\tfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\n11\tfrom sigil.bridge.daemon import BridgeDaemon\n12\tfrom sigil.gesture.components import RecordingInputBackend\n13\tfrom sigil.gesture.session import (MAX_DEVICE_TTL, SESSION_ARMED, SessionGate, arm_request_message,\n14\t                                    pending_device_arm, sign_arm_request)\n15\tfrom sigil.gesture.types import GestureIntent\n16\tfrom sigil.mesh import authorize_device, revoke_device\n17\tfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair, verify_one\n18\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n19\t\n20\tOWNER = generate_keypair()\n21\tOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n22\tDEV = generate_keypair()          # a phone the owner will authorize\n23\tATTACKER = generate_keypair()     # a device the owner NEVER authorized\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef _store():\n27\t    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tclass FakeCls:\n31\t    \"\"\"hid.pointer.* → A1 (inject in-session); hid.type/combo/launch → A2 (queue). No kernel subprocess.\"\"\"\n32\t    def classify(self, tool):\n33\t        return Tier.A1 if tool.startswith(\"hid.pointer\") else Tier.A2\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _gate(store, backend=None):\n37\t    return SessionGate(store, backend or RecordingInputBackend(), classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n38\t\n39\t\n40\tdef _authorize(store, kp=DEV, device_id=\"phone1\"):\n41\t    authorize_device(store, device_id, kp.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _req(kp=DEV, *, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=1, ts=None, ttl=120.0):\n45\t    return sign_arm_request(kp, device_id=device_id, nonce=nonce, ts=ts if ts is not None else time.time(), ttl_seconds=ttl)\n46\t\n47\t\n48\t# ---- happy path + TTL clamp ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\tdef test_authorized_device_arms_and_ttl_is_clamped():\n50\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n51\t    now = time.time()\n52\t    sess = g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now, ttl=120.0), now=now)\n53\t    assert sess is not None and sess.live, \"an authorized, fresh, signed request arms a session\"\n54\t    assert abs(sess.expires_at - (now + 120.0)) < 1e-6, \"ttl within MAX is honored\"\n55\t    # a request over the cap clamps to MAX_DEVICE_TTL (a trust-narrowing inside the widening)\n56\t    s2 = _store(); _authorize(s2); g2 = _gate(s2)\n57\t    sess2 = g2.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now, ttl=99999.0), now=now)\n58\t    assert abs(sess2.expires_at - (now + MAX_DEVICE_TTL)) < 1e-6, f\"ttl clamped to {MAX_DEVICE_TTL}\"\n59\t\n60\t\n61\t# ---- the threat table (each refusal records a refusal + returns None + arms nothing) --------------\n62\tdef test_forged_device_cannot_arm():\n63\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s)                    # DEV authorized, ATTACKER is not\n64\t    g = _gate(s)\n65\t    now = time.time()\n66\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(kp=ATTACKER, ts=now), now=now) is None, \"an unauthorized device cannot arm\"\n67\t    assert g.session is None\n68\t    assert any(r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"gesture.arm_request\" and r.payload.get(\"decision\") == \"refused\"\n69\t               for r in s.iter_records()), \"the refusal is recorded\"\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tdef test_tampered_signature_refused():\n73\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n74\t    now = time.time()\n75\t    req = _req(ts=now)\n76\t    req[\"ttl_seconds\"] = 999.0                      # mutate a SIGNED field after signing → sig no longer matches\n77\t    assert g.arm_by_device(req, now=now) is None and g.session is None, \"a tampered arm request is refused\"\n78\t\n79\t\n80\tdef test_replayed_arm_nonce_refused():\n81\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n82\t    now = time.time()\n83\t    req = _req(nonce=7, ts=now)\n84\t    assert g.arm_by_device(req, now=now) is not None, \"first arm with nonce 7 succeeds\"\n85\t    g.disarm()\n86\t    assert g.arm_by_device(req, now=now) is None, \"re-submitting the SAME (device,nonce) is refused (replay)\"\n87\t\n88\t\n89\tdef test_stale_arm_refused():\n90\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n91\t    now = time.time()\n92\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=now - 100.0), now=now) is None, \"a stale (old-ts) arm request is refused\"\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tdef test_arm_after_revoke_refused():\n96\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n97\t    now = time.time()\n98\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now), now=now) is not None, \"authorized device arms\"\n99\t    g.disarm()\n100\t    revoke_device(s, \"phone1\", DEV.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n101\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=2, ts=now), now=now) is None, \"a REVOKED device can no longer arm\"\n102\t\n103\t\n104\tdef test_killswitch_blocks_arm():\n105\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n106\t    BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).panic_engage(by=\"test\")     # owner/phone panic\n107\t    now = time.time()\n108\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=now), now=now) is None, \"no arm while the kill-switch is engaged\"\n109\t\n110\t\n111\tdef test_single_session_not_displaced_by_device_arm():\n112\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n113\t    now = time.time()\n114\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now), now=now) is not None, \"first arm succeeds\"\n115\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=2, ts=now), now=now) is None, \"a device arm never displaces a live session\"\n116\t\n117\t\n118\t# ---- owner always wins + downstream bounds unchanged ---------------------------------------------\n119\tdef test_owner_disarm_beats_a_device_armed_session():\n120\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); b = RecordingInputBackend(); g = _gate(s, b)\n121\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=time.time()), now=time.time())\n122\t    g.disarm()\n123\t    v = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=0.5, dy=0.5))\n124\t    assert not v[\"injected\"] and b.calls == [], \"after owner disarm, a device-armed session injects nothing\"\n125\t\n126\t\n127\tdef test_killswitch_neuters_a_device_armed_session_mid_flight():\n128\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); b = RecordingInputBackend(); g = _gate(s, b)\n129\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=time.time()), now=time.time())\n130\t    BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).panic_engage(by=\"test\")     # owner panic AFTER arming\n131\t    v = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=0.5, dy=0.5))\n132\t    assert not v[\"injected\"] and \"kill-switch\" in v[\"reason\"], \"an owner panic neuters injection mid-session\"\n133\t    assert b.calls == [] and g.session is None, \"the session is disarmed on the kill-switch\"\n134\t\n135\t\n136\tdef test_device_armed_session_still_bounds_to_a1_and_queues_type():\n137\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); b = RecordingInputBackend(); g = _gate(s, b)\n138\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=time.time()), now=time.time())\n139\t    mv = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=0.5, dy=0.5))\n140\t    assert mv[\"injected\"] and mv[\"tier\"] == \"A1\", \"a pointer move auto-injects (A1) in a device-armed session\"\n141\t    ty = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"type\", arg=\"secret\"))\n142\t    assert not ty[\"injected\"] and ty.get(\"queued\") is not None, \"type NEVER auto-injects — it QUEUES (A2), even device-armed\"\n143\t\n144\t\n145\t# ---- the arm record is self-verifying + the daemon transport gate ---------------------------------\n146\tdef test_arm_record_is_self_verifying_against_the_device_ledger():\n147\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n148\t    now = time.time()\n149\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=42, ts=now, ttl=120.0), now=now)\n150\t    rec = next(r for r in s.iter_records()\n151\t               if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and r.payload.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\")\n152\t    p = rec.payload\n153\t    core = {\"signal\": \"gesture.arm_request\", \"device_id\": p[\"device_id\"], \"nonce\": p[\"nonce\"],\n154\t            \"ts\": p[\"ts\"], \"ttl_seconds\": p[\"ttl_seconds\"]}\n155\t    assert verify_one(p[\"device_pubkey\"], arm_request_message(core), p[\"sig\"]), \\\n156\t        \"the armed record's authorization re-verifies against the device signature (tamper-evident)\"\n157\t\n158\t\n159\tdef test_daemon_submit_arm_request_gates_on_authorization():\n160\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s)\n161\t    d = BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP)\n162\t    now = time.time()\n163\t    seq = d.submit_arm_request(_req(ts=now))\n164\t    assert isinstance(seq, int), \"an authorized, signed arm request is recorded\"\n165\t    try:\n166\t        d.submit_arm_request(_req(kp=ATTACKER, ts=now))\n167\t        assert False, \"an unauthorized device's arm request must be refused\"\n168\t    except ValueError:\n169\t        pass\n170\t    bad = _req(ts=now); bad[\"sig\"] = \"AAAA\"          # forged/garbage signature\n171\t    try:\n172\t        d.submit_arm_request(bad)\n173\t        assert False, \"a bad signature must be refused\"\n174\t    except ValueError:\n175\t        pass\n176\t\n177\t\n178\t# ---- review fixes: NaN safety-bound bypass, clamp-self-verify, end-to-end consumption --------------\n179\tdef test_nan_ts_and_ttl_are_refused():                            # red-pen BLOCK-1\n180\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n181\t    now = time.time()\n182\t    assert g.arm_by_device(sign_arm_request(DEV, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=1, ts=float(\"nan\"), ttl_seconds=120.0), now=now) is None, \\\n183\t        \"a signed NaN ts is refused (freshness is fail-closed for non-finite input)\"\n184\t    assert g.arm_by_device(sign_arm_request(DEV, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=2, ts=now, ttl_seconds=float(\"nan\")), now=now) is None, \\\n185\t        \"a signed NaN ttl is refused (no never-expiring session)\"\n186\t    assert g.arm_by_device(sign_arm_request(DEV, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=3, ts=now, ttl_seconds=float(\"inf\")), now=now) is None, \\\n187\t        \"a signed +Inf ttl is refused\"\n188\t    assert g.session is None, \"no non-finite request ever arms\"\n189\t\n190\t\n191\tdef test_arm_record_self_verifies_even_when_ttl_is_clamped():     # red-pen BLOCK-2\n192\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n193\t    now = time.time()\n194\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=5, ts=now, ttl=99999.0), now=now)  # requested 99999 → clamped to MAX\n195\t    rec = next(r for r in s.iter_records()\n196\t               if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and r.payload.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\")\n197\t    p = rec.payload\n198\t    assert p[\"effective_ttl\"] == MAX_DEVICE_TTL and p[\"ttl_seconds\"] == 99999.0, \\\n199\t        \"the record keeps the ORIGINAL signed ttl AND the clamped effective ttl\"\n200\t    core = {\"signal\": \"gesture.arm_request\", \"device_id\": p[\"device_id\"], \"nonce\": p[\"nonce\"],\n201\t            \"ts\": p[\"ts\"], \"ttl_seconds\": p[\"ttl_seconds\"]}\n202\t    assert verify_one(p[\"device_pubkey\"], arm_request_message(core), p[\"sig\"]), \\\n203\t        \"the armed record re-verifies against the device signature EVEN when the enforced TTL was clamped\"\n204\t\n205\t\n206\tdef test_recorded_arm_request_is_consumed_exactly_once():         # HIGH-3 consumption path\n207\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s)\n208\t    BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).submit_arm_request(_req(nonce=9, ts=time.time()))\n209\t    req = pending_device_arm(s, OP)\n210\t    assert req is not None and req[\"pubkey\"] == DEV.public_key_b64, \"a recorded request is a pending candidate\"\n211\t    g = _gate(s)\n212\t    assert g.arm_by_device(req, now=time.time()) is not None, \"the daemon consumes it → armed\"\n213\t    assert pending_device_arm(s, OP) is None, \"once armed it is no longer pending (never double-consumed)\"\n214\t\n215\t\n216\tdef test_run_gesture_device_arm_activates_from_a_recorded_request():   # HIGH-3 end-to-end\n217\t    from sigil.gesture.components import ScriptedLandmarker\n218\t    from sigil.gesture.features import RuleClassifier\n219\t    from sigil.gesture.run import run_gesture\n220\t    from sigil.perception.camera_stream import ScriptedFrameSource\n221\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s)\n222\t    BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).submit_arm_request(_req(nonce=1, ts=time.time()))\n223\t    b = RecordingInputBackend()\n224\t    g = SessionGate(s, b, classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n225\t    run_gesture(store=s, source=ScriptedFrameSource([None, None, None]),\n226\t                landmarker=ScriptedLandmarker([[], [], []]), classifier=RuleClassifier(),\n227\t                backend=b, gate=g, auto_arm=False, device_arm=True, trusted_pubkey=OP, max_frames=3)\n228\t    assert any(r.payload.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and r.payload.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\"\n229\t               for r in s.iter_records()), \\\n230\t        \"run_gesture(device_arm=True) consumed the recorded request and ARMED end-to-end (feature is not inert)\"\n231\t\n232\t\n233\tdef test_run_gesture_device_arm_does_not_respam_a_stale_request():   # fix-introduced-defect guard\n234\t    from sigil.gesture.components import ScriptedLandmarker\n235\t    from sigil.gesture.features import RuleClassifier\n236\t    from sigil.gesture.run import run_gesture\n237\t    from sigil.perception.camera_stream import ScriptedFrameSource\n238\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s)\n239\t    BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).submit_arm_request(_req(nonce=1, ts=time.time() - 10_000))  # STALE\n240\t    b = RecordingInputBackend()\n241\t    g = SessionGate(s, b, classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n242\t    run_gesture(store=s, source=ScriptedFrameSource([None] * 6), landmarker=ScriptedLandmarker([[]] * 6),\n243\t                classifier=RuleClassifier(), backend=b, gate=g, auto_arm=False, device_arm=True,\n244\t                trusted_pubkey=OP, max_frames=6)\n245\t    refusals = sum(1 for r in s.iter_records()\n246\t                   if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"gesture.arm_request\" and r.payload.get(\"decision\") == \"refused\")\n247\t    assert refusals <= 1, f\"a stale recorded request is attempted at most ONCE (no per-frame refusal spam), got {refusals}\"\n248\t    assert not any(r.payload.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\" for r in s.iter_records()), \"the stale request never armed\"\n249\t\n250\t\n251\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n252\t    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith(\"test_\")]\n253\t    passed = 0\n254\t    for fn in fns:\n255\t        try:\n256\t            fn(); print(f\"  PASS  {fn.__name__}\"); passed += 1\n257\t        except AssertionError as e:\n258\t            print(f\"  FAIL  {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n259\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n260\t            import traceback\n261\t            print(f\"  ERROR {fn.__name__}: {e}\")\n262\t            traceback.print_exc()\n263\t    print(f\"{passed}/{len(fns)} Phase-9 W2-K (device-signed remote arm) guarantees hold\")\n264\t"}]},"uuid":"1798c887-fb41-460f-8400-34ec99849001","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:46:04.928Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c95e5f45-4f2b-49b5-a4b8-ec2590df96c0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"2179917b-2cd9-420f-9ba3-2157f78dd630","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DtEHXZBP7iPSGZmtvYyp5X","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"1aee2bc4-d580-4962-9158-6f4ac98fb8b6","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:46:12.647Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2179917b-2cd9-420f-9ba3-2157f78dd630","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"7441132f-0549-47aa-be32-e8f2e8049364","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d3668ff5-e53f-4988-a0bf-84757939e1af","agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MJvky339mTpRj5AFkGTwCi","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL bridge server (Phase 9 W1-B) — the WireGuard-bound HTTP transport that lets an\n2\tAUTHORIZED phone reach the desktop `BridgeDaemon`. It forks the WS-C glass-cockpit server\n3\t(`sigil.ui.server`) verbatim in shape (ThreadingHTTPServer + BaseHTTPRequestHandler, the strict\n4\tCSP/nosniff/no-referrer header set, the body cap + 30s timeout, the never-log-auth `log_message`,\n5\tthe SSE structure, the anti-DNS-rebinding Host/Origin/Referer gate) and changes ONLY three things:\n6\t\n7\t  • THE BIND. It binds a `bind_ok` address (loopback or a PRIVATE/WireGuard address) — NEVER\n8\t    0.0.0.0 / a public address. The constructor asserts this; the CLI asserts it too. This is the\n9\t    non-negotiable exposure guard: the tunnel, not the transport, is the network boundary.\n10\t\n11\t  • THE AUTH. There is NO wire bearer secret (the ui's printed token is gone). Authentication IS a\n12\t    per-request Ed25519 signature the phone makes with ITS OWN owner-authorized device key — the\n13\t    Wave-1 device envelope (`bridge.envelope`). The server verifies the signature against the\n14\t    owner-minted authorized-device set (recomputed PER REQUEST so a revocation takes effect at once),\n15\t    binds the authenticated envelope `action` to the endpoint it hit (a `read:pending` envelope can\n16\t    never reach the more-sensitive `read:recall`), applies a wallclock timestamp-freshness window\n17\t    (injectable clock), and for EFFECTFUL actions (panic/relay) additionally runs the envelope's\n18\t    strict monotonic-nonce replay gate (`consume(effectful=True)`). The owner trust-root is NEVER used\n19\t    to sign anything here — the phone signs, the server only verifies.\n20\t\n21\t  • THE ALLOWLIST. The anti-rebind Host/Origin allowlist is derived from the REAL bound address (plus\n22\t    the loopback pair when bound to loopback, for dev), not a hardcoded 127.0.0.1.\n23\t\n24\tEverything sensitive stays off the minimal frames: `/api/pending` and the SSE stream carry only\n25\t`{seq, tier, kind}` — never a subject, never a payload, never a secret. `serve()` now wraps the\n26\ttransport in an owner-PINNED self-signed TLS cert (the phone PWA needs a secure context — https or\n27\tlocalhost — to install and register a service worker; over a bare WireGuard IP that means self-signed\n28\tTLS whose sha256 fingerprint the owner pins once, stable across restarts). `build_server` itself\n29\tstays plain HTTP so the in-tunnel test harness is unchanged and TLS can be terminated upstream if\n30\tpreferred. Offense-free.\"\"\"\n31\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n32\t\n33\timport base64\n34\timport ipaddress\n35\timport json\n36\timport os\n37\timport time\n38\tfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\n39\tfrom pathlib import Path\n40\tfrom urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse\n41\t\n42\tfrom ..config import SPINE_PATH\n43\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n44\tfrom ..mesh import authorized_devices\n45\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n46\tfrom ..spine.verify import verify_record\n47\tfrom .daemon import BridgeDaemon, bind_ok\n48\tfrom .envelope import consume, verify_envelope\n49\tfrom .notifier import PushNotifier\n50\t\n51\t_WEBAPP = Path(__file__).parent / \"webapp\"       # the PWA is a later slice — served if present, else 404\n52\t_CSP = \"default-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'\"\n53\t_DEFAULT_PORT = 8722\n54\t_TS_WINDOW = 120.0                               # ± seconds a request timestamp may differ from the server clock\n55\t\n56\t# Each network endpoint declares the ONE envelope action that authorizes it. Binding the\n57\t# authenticated action to the endpoint stops a captured lesser-scoped envelope (e.g. `read:pending`,\n58\t# which leaks only {seq,tier,kind}) from being replayed against a more-sensitive endpoint (e.g.\n59\t# `read:recall`, which surfaces the owner's on-screen OCR history). `/api/graph` piggybacks on the\n60\t# `read:snapshot` scope: graph returns only aggregate health, strictly LESS sensitive than the\n61\t# snapshot, so a snapshot-scoped envelope reaching it is not an escalation (and a lesser envelope\n62\t# still cannot reach it).\n63\t_READ_ACTION = {\n64\t    \"/api/pending\": \"read:pending\",\n65\t    \"/api/snapshot\": \"read:snapshot\",\n66\t    \"/api/graph\": \"read:snapshot\",\n67\t    \"/api/stream\": \"read:stream\",\n68\t    \"/api/recall\": \"read:recall\",\n69\t}\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tclass BridgeServer(ThreadingHTTPServer):\n73\t    daemon_threads = True\n74\t\n75\t    def __init__(self, addr, handler, *, spine_path, trusted_pubkey=None, clock=None,\n76\t                 ts_window: float = _TS_WINDOW):\n77\t        host = addr[0]\n78\t        if not bind_ok(host):\n79\t            raise ValueError(\n80\t                f\"refusing to bind {host!r}: the bridge binds loopback or a PRIVATE (WireGuard) \"\n81\t                f\"address only — never 0.0.0.0 / an unspecified / a public address\")\n82\t        super().__init__(addr, handler)\n83\t        self.spine_path = Path(spine_path)\n84\t        # the owner PUBLIC key is the trust anchor (injectable for tests); NO private key ever lives here\n85\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n86\t        self.clock = clock or time.time          # injectable wallclock so the freshness gate is deterministic\n87\t        self.ts_window = ts_window\n88\t        port = self.server_address[1]            # the ACTUAL bound port (correct even for port 0)\n89\t        hosts = {f\"{host}:{port}\"}\n90\t        origins = {f\"http://{host}:{port}\"}\n91\t        if ipaddress.ip_address(host).is_loopback:   # dev convenience only — not added for a WG bind\n92\t            hosts |= {f\"127.0.0.1:{port}\", f\"localhost:{port}\"}\n93\t            origins |= {f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}\", f\"http://localhost:{port}\"}\n94\t        self.allowed_hosts = frozenset(hosts)\n95\t        self.allowed_origins = frozenset(origins)\n96\t\n97\t    def store(self) -> SpineStore:\n98\t        return SpineStore(self.spine_path)       # fresh read each request (cheap, current)\n99\t\n100\t    def daemon(self) -> BridgeDaemon:\n101\t        return BridgeDaemon(self.store(), trusted_pubkey=self.trusted_pubkey)\n102\t\n103\t\n104\tdef _json_bytes(obj) -> bytes:\n105\t    return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False).encode(\"utf-8\")\n106\t\n107\t\n108\tclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n109\t    server_version = \"sigil-bridge/1.0\"\n110\t    timeout = 30                                  # per-connection socket timeout (no hung reader)\n111\t    _MAX_BODY = 65536                             # bodies are tiny; cap to avoid a Content-Length hang/alloc\n112\t\n113\t    # never log auth (an envelope can ride in ?env= for SSE; never write it to a log)\n114\t    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):  # noqa: A003\n115\t        pass\n116\t\n117\t    # --- request helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n118\t    def _query(self) -> dict:\n119\t        return parse_qs(urlparse(self.path).query)\n120\t\n121\t    def _authorized_now(self):\n122\t        \"\"\"The owner-minted authorized-device set, recomputed PER REQUEST — a revoke takes effect now.\"\"\"\n123\t        return authorized_devices(self.server.store(), self.server.trusted_pubkey)\n124\t\n125\t    def _envelope_payload(self):\n126\t        \"\"\"The device envelope: base64url of canonical JSON, in `X-SIGIL-Envelope` (GET/POST) or the\n127\t        `?env=` query (SSE, which cannot set a header). Returns the payload dict, or None if absent/\n128\t        malformed (fail-closed — the caller denies).\"\"\"\n129\t        raw = self.headers.get(\"X-SIGIL-Envelope\") or (self._query().get(\"env\", [\"\"])[0])\n130\t        if not raw:\n131\t            return None\n132\t        try:\n133\t            data = base64.urlsafe_b64decode(raw + \"=\" * (-len(raw) % 4))\n134\t            payload = json.loads(data)\n135\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — any decode/parse failure is an unauthenticated request\n136\t            return None\n137\t        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else None\n138\t\n139\t    def _fresh(self, core) -> bool:\n140\t        \"\"\"The server-layer wallclock freshness window (the envelope module is pure — it never reads a\n141\t        clock — so freshness lives HERE). A non-numeric / far-off timestamp fails closed.\"\"\"\n142\t        try:\n143\t            ts = float(core.get(\"ts\"))\n144\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n145\t            return False\n146\t        return abs(self.server.clock() - ts) <= self.server.ts_window\n147\t\n148\t    def _authed(self, action: str):\n149\t        \"\"\"Authenticate a READ: verify the device signature, apply freshness, bind the action to this\n150\t        endpoint. Reads are side-effect-free, so they are NOT receipted (no spine write per GET) — the\n151\t        freshness window is their replay bound. Returns the core, or None after sending a deny.\"\"\"\n152\t        payload = self._envelope_payload()\n153\t        if payload is None:\n154\t            self._deny(401, \"missing/invalid device envelope\")\n155\t            return None\n156\t        ok, core = verify_envelope(payload, self._authorized_now())\n157\t        if not ok:\n158\t            self._deny(401, f\"unauthenticated: {core}\")\n159\t            return None\n160\t        if not self._fresh(core):\n161\t            self._deny(401, \"stale request (timestamp outside freshness window)\")\n162\t            return None\n163\t        if core.get(\"action\") != action:\n164\t            self._deny(403, \"envelope action does not authorize this endpoint\")\n165\t            return None\n166\t        return core\n167\t\n168\t    def _authed_effectful(self, action: str):\n169\t        \"\"\"Authenticate an EFFECTFUL request (panic/relay): verify + freshness + endpoint-bind, then run\n170\t        the envelope's strict monotonic-nonce replay gate and receipt it on the spine\n171\t        (`consume(effectful=True)`). A replayed/stale-nonce envelope is refused. Returns core or None.\"\"\"\n172\t        payload = self._envelope_payload()\n173\t        if payload is None:\n174\t            self._deny(401, \"missing/invalid device envelope\")\n175\t            return None\n176\t        authorized = self._authorized_now()\n177\t        ok, core = verify_envelope(payload, authorized)\n178\t        if not ok:\n179\t            self._deny(401, f\"unauthenticated: {core}\")\n180\t            return None\n181\t        if not self._fresh(core):\n182\t            self._deny(401, \"stale request (timestamp outside freshness window)\")\n183\t            return None\n184\t        if core.get(\"action\") != action:\n185\t            self._deny(403, \"envelope action does not authorize this endpoint\")\n186\t            return None\n187\t        try:\n188\t            return consume(self.server.store(), payload, authorized, effectful=True)\n189\t        except ValueError as e:                   # replay: nonce not fresh (fail-closed)\n190\t            self._deny(409, f\"refused: {str(e)[:120]}\")\n191\t            return None\n192\t\n193\t    def _rebind_ok(self) -> bool:\n194\t        \"\"\"The anti-DNS-rebinding gate (ui `_action_ok` logic, minus the token — the envelope is the\n195\t        credential): the `Host` must be in the WG-derived allowlist, an `Origin`, if present, must\n196\t        EXACT-match an allowed origin (a prefix like `http://IP:PORT.evil.com` must NOT pass), and a\n197\t        `Referer`, if present, must sit under an allowed origin. Applied to the whole POST action\n198\t        plane as defense-in-depth.\"\"\"\n199\t        if self.headers.get(\"Host\", \"\") not in self.server.allowed_hosts:\n200\t            return False\n201\t        o = self.headers.get(\"Origin\") or \"\"\n202\t        ref = self.headers.get(\"Referer\") or \"\"\n203\t        if o and o not in self.server.allowed_origins:\n204\t            return False\n205\t        if ref and not any(ref.startswith(a + \"/\") or ref == a for a in self.server.allowed_origins):\n206\t            return False\n207\t        return True\n208\t\n209\t    # --- response helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n210\t    def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype=\"application/json\"):\n211\t        self.send_response(code)\n212\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", ctype)\n213\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Length\", str(len(body)))\n214\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Security-Policy\", _CSP)\n215\t        self.send_header(\"X-Content-Type-Options\", \"nosniff\")\n216\t        self.send_header(\"Referrer-Policy\", \"no-referrer\")\n217\t        self.end_headers()\n218\t        try:\n219\t            self.wfile.write(body)\n220\t        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):\n221\t            pass\n222\t\n223\t    def _json(self, obj, code=200):\n224\t        self._send(code, _json_bytes(obj))\n225\t\n226\t    def _deny(self, code=403, msg=\"forbidden\"):\n227\t        self._json({\"error\": msg}, code)\n228\t\n229\t    # --- GET (read plane) -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n230\t    def do_GET(self):\n231\t        path = urlparse(self.path).path\n232\t        if path in (\"/\", \"/index.html\"):\n233\t            return self._serve_index()\n234\t        if path.startswith(\"/static/\"):\n235\t            return self._serve_static(path[len(\"/static/\"):])\n236\t        if not path.startswith(\"/api/\"):\n237\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n238\t        if path == \"/api/stream\":\n239\t            return self._sse()                    # SSE authenticates via ?env= inside _authed\n240\t        if path == \"/api/pending\":\n241\t            if self._authed(_READ_ACTION[path]) is None:\n242\t                return\n243\t            return self._json({\"pending\": self.server.daemon().pending()})   # {seq,tier,kind} only\n244\t        if path == \"/api/snapshot\":\n245\t            if self._authed(_READ_ACTION[path]) is None:\n246\t                return\n247\t            from ..dashboard import snapshot\n248\t            return self._json(snapshot(self.server.store()))\n249\t        if path == \"/api/graph\":\n250\t            if self._authed(_READ_ACTION[path]) is None:\n251\t                return\n252\t            return self._graph()\n253\t        if path.startswith(\"/api/record/\"):\n254\t            if self._authed(\"read:record\") is None:\n255\t                return\n256\t            return self._record(path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1])\n257\t        if path == \"/api/recall\":\n258\t            if self._authed(_READ_ACTION[path]) is None:\n259\t                return\n260\t            subject = self._query().get(\"subject\", [\"\"])[0]\n261\t            return self._json({\"subject\": subject, \"recall\": self.server.daemon().recall(subject)})\n262\t        return self._deny(404, \"unknown endpoint\")\n263\t\n264\t    def _serve_index(self):\n265\t        idx = _WEBAPP / \"index.html\"\n266\t        if not idx.is_file():\n267\t            return self._deny(404, \"bridge PWA not built yet\")   # graceful — the webapp is a later slice\n268\t        try:\n269\t            self._send(200, idx.read_bytes(), ctype=\"text/html; charset=utf-8\")\n270\t        except OSError:\n271\t            self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n272\t\n273\t    def _serve_static(self, sub: str):\n274\t        base = _WEBAPP.resolve()\n275\t        target = (base / sub).resolve()\n276\t        if base != target and base not in target.parents:        # traversal guard (allowlist by containment)\n277\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n278\t        if not target.is_file():\n279\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")                  # includes: webapp dir absent (later slice)\n280\t        name = target.name\n281\t        ctype = (\"application/javascript\" if name.endswith(\".js\")\n282\t                 else \"text/css\" if name.endswith(\".css\")\n283\t                 else \"text/html\" if name.endswith(\".html\")\n284\t                 else \"application/octet-stream\")\n285\t        try:\n286\t            self._send(200, target.read_bytes(), ctype=f\"{ctype}; charset=utf-8\")\n287\t        except OSError:\n288\t            self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n289\t\n290\t    def _record(self, raw):\n291\t        try:\n292\t            seq = int(raw)\n293\t        except ValueError:\n294\t            return self._deny(400, \"bad seq\")\n295\t        rec = self.server.store().get(seq)\n296\t        if rec is None:\n297\t            return self._json({\"error\": \"no such record\", \"seq\": seq,\n298\t                               \"note\": \"no grounded record — not fabricated\"}, 404)\n299\t        ok, reason = verify_record(rec)                          # re-verify the atom LIVE (prove-don't-guess)\n300\t        self._json({\"seq\": rec.seq, \"kind\": rec.kind, \"source\": rec.source, \"actor\": rec.actor,\n301\t                    \"ts\": rec.ts, \"entry_hash\": rec.entry_hash, \"prev_hash\": rec.prev_hash,\n302\t                    \"payload\": rec.payload, \"integrity_ok\": ok, \"integrity_reason\": reason})\n303\t\n304\t    def _graph(self):\n305\t        try:\n306\t            from ..graph import health\n307\t            self._json({\"health\": health()})\n308\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — graph may not be built yet (ImportError included)\n309\t            self._json({\"error\": \"graph unavailable\", \"note\": str(e)[:200]})\n310\t\n311\t    def _sse(self):\n312\t        if self._authed(\"read:stream\") is None:                  # deny already sent (401/403)\n313\t            return\n314\t        self.send_response(200)\n315\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"text/event-stream\")\n316\t        self.send_header(\"Cache-Control\", \"no-cache\")\n317\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Security-Policy\", _CSP)\n318\t        self.send_header(\"X-Content-Type-Options\", \"nosniff\")\n319\t        self.send_header(\"Referrer-Policy\", \"no-referrer\")\n320\t        self.end_headers()\n321\t        try:\n322\t            since = int(self._query().get(\"since\", [\"-1\"])[0])\n323\t        except (ValueError, TypeError):\n324\t            since = -1                                            # any malformed cursor → from genesis\n325\t        notifier = PushNotifier(self.server.store(), since_seq=since)\n326\t        try:\n327\t            while True:\n328\t                sent = False\n329\t                for ev in notifier.poll():\n330\t                    frame = {\"seq\": ev[\"seq\"], \"tier\": ev[\"tier\"], \"kind\": ev[\"kind\"]}   # minimal — no subject\n331\t                    self.wfile.write(f\"data: {json.dumps(frame, ensure_ascii=False)}\\n\\n\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n332\t                    sent = True\n333\t                self.wfile.write(b\": hb\\n\\n\")                     # heartbeat / flush\n334\t                self.wfile.flush()\n335\t                if not sent:\n336\t                    time.sleep(0.25)\n337\t        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError, OSError):\n338\t            return                                                # client closed — end the stream\n339\t\n340\t    # --- POST (action plane) ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n341\t    def do_POST(self):\n342\t        path = urlparse(self.path).path\n343\t        if path not in (\"/api/action\", \"/api/panic\", \"/api/relay\", \"/api/gesture/arm\"):\n344\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n345\t        length = int(self.headers.get(\"Content-Length\", \"0\") or \"0\")\n346\t        if length > self._MAX_BODY:                              # cap the body (no CL hang / alloc)\n347\t            return self._deny(413, \"body too large\")\n348\t        body_bytes = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b\"\"\n349\t        if not self._rebind_ok():                               # anti-rebind gate on the whole action plane\n350\t            return self._deny(403, \"action denied (origin / host — possible DNS rebinding)\")\n351\t        if path == \"/api/action\":\n352\t            return self._device_action(body_bytes)\n353\t        if path == \"/api/gesture/arm\":\n354\t            return self._device_arm(body_bytes)\n355\t        # panic / relay — effectful, envelope-authenticated with the strict nonce replay gate\n356\t        action = \"panic\" if path == \"/api/panic\" else \"relay\"\n357\t        core = self._authed_effectful(action)\n358\t        if core is None:\n359\t            return\n360\t        if action == \"panic\":\n361\t            return self._json({\"ok\": True, \"seq\": self.server.daemon().panic_engage(by=\"phone\")})\n362\t        text = str((core.get(\"args\") or {}).get(\"text\", \"\"))     # the relayed command is INSIDE the signed core\n363\t        return self._json({\"ok\": True, \"reply\": self.server.daemon().relay(text)})\n364\t\n365\t    def _device_action(self, body_bytes):\n366\t        \"\"\"The phone posts its OWN device-signed `governor.approval` payload. The SERVER only VERIFIES\n367\t        (it never signs) — `submit_device_approval` accepts it iff the signing device is currently\n368\t        authorized AND the signature verifies; the signed `target_seq` binds, so no nonce is needed.\"\"\"\n369\t        try:\n370\t            body = json.loads(body_bytes or b\"{}\")\n371\t        except (ValueError, TypeError) as e:\n372\t            return self._deny(400, f\"bad request: {e}\")\n373\t        if not isinstance(body, dict):\n374\t            return self._deny(400, \"bad request: body must be a JSON object\")\n375\t        try:\n376\t            seq = self.server.daemon().submit_device_approval(body)\n377\t        except ValueError as e:                                  # unauthorized / forged / not-an-approval\n378\t            return self._deny(403, f\"approval refused: {str(e)[:200]}\")\n379\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — never leak internals as a 500\n380\t            return self._deny(400, f\"action failed: {str(e)[:200]}\")\n381\t        self._json({\"ok\": True, \"seq\": seq})\n382\t\n383\t    def _device_arm(self, body_bytes):\n384\t        \"\"\"The phone posts its OWN device-signed gesture arm request. The SERVER only VERIFIES + RECORDS\n385\t        it (`submit_arm_request`, fail-closed on unauthorized/forged); the live SessionGate re-verifies\n386\t        and enforces freshness / kill-switch / single-session / TTL when it consumes it. Recording is\n387\t        necessary-but-not-sufficient to arm — the trust boundary is `arm_by_device`, not this endpoint.\"\"\"\n388\t        try:\n389\t            body = json.loads(body_bytes or b\"{}\")\n390\t        except (ValueError, TypeError) as e:\n391\t            return self._deny(400, f\"bad request: {e}\")\n392\t        if not isinstance(body, dict):\n393\t            return self._deny(400, \"bad request: body must be a JSON object\")\n394\t        try:\n395\t            seq = self.server.daemon().submit_arm_request(body)\n396\t        except ValueError as e:                                  # unauthorized / forged / not-an-arm-request\n397\t            return self._deny(403, f\"arm refused: {str(e)[:200]}\")\n398\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — never leak internals as a 500\n399\t            return self._deny(400, f\"arm failed: {str(e)[:200]}\")\n400\t        self._json({\"ok\": True, \"seq\": seq})\n401\t\n402\t\n403\tdef build_server(*, addr: str, port: int = _DEFAULT_PORT, spine_path=None, trusted_pubkey=None,\n404\t                 clock=None) -> BridgeServer:\n405\t    \"\"\"Build (but do not run) the bridge server bound to `addr` (asserted `bind_ok`). `trusted_pubkey`\n406\t    and `clock` are injectable for deterministic tests; both default to the real owner identity / wallclock.\"\"\"\n407\t    return BridgeServer((addr, port), Handler,\n408\t                        spine_path=Path(spine_path) if spine_path else SPINE_PATH,\n409\t                        trusted_pubkey=trusted_pubkey, clock=clock)\n410\t\n411\t\n412\tdef _write_secure(path: Path, data: bytes) -> None:\n413\t    \"\"\"Write `data` to `path` created 0600 up-front (no world-readable window) — the vault/secrets\n414\t    secure-file idiom (`os.open(..., 0o600)`).\"\"\"\n415\t    fd = os.open(str(path), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)\n416\t    with os.fdopen(fd, \"wb\") as fh:\n417\t        fh.write(data)\n418\t\n419\t\n420\tdef _fingerprint(cert) -> str:\n421\t    \"\"\"The pinned identity: sha256 of the DER cert, hex, colon-grouped byte pairs (openssl-style,\n422\t    upper-case) — what the owner eyeball-matches on the phone once.\"\"\"\n423\t    from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes\n424\t    h = cert.fingerprint(hashes.SHA256()).hex().upper()      # fingerprint() == sha256 of the DER encoding\n425\t    return \":\".join(h[i:i + 2] for i in range(0, len(h), 2))\n426\t\n427\t\n428\tdef ensure_bridge_cert(addr: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n429\t    \"\"\"Mint (or REUSE) a minimal owner-pinned self-signed TLS cert for the bridge bound to `addr`,\n430\t    returning `(certfile, keyfile, sha256_fingerprint)`.\n431\t\n432\t    The cert carries `addr` as its CN and (when `addr` is an IP — always true for a `bind_ok` bridge)\n433\t    as an `x509.IPAddress` SubjectAlternativeName, so an IP-literal TLS connection matches. Cert+key\n434\t    live under `SIGIL_HOME/bridge/` (dir 0700, files 0600). If a cert already exists FOR THIS ADDR it\n435\t    is REUSED — the fingerprint is therefore STABLE across restarts, so the owner pins it exactly once\n436\t    (a changed fingerprint later means a MITM / a wiped key, not a benign restart). An EC P-256 key is\n437\t    used (easy for `cryptography`, accepted by `ssl`). Validity ~2 years; a real wallclock is fine here\n438\t    (this is TLS notBefore/notAfter, not spine or learning math).\"\"\"\n439\t    import datetime\n440\t    import ipaddress as _ip\n441\t\n442\t    from cryptography import x509\n443\t    from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes, serialization\n444\t    from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import ec\n445\t    from cryptography.x509.oid import NameOID\n446\t\n447\t    from ..config import SIGIL_HOME                          # read at call-time (patchable in tests)\n448\t\n449\t    bridge_dir = Path(SIGIL_HOME) / \"bridge\"\n450\t    bridge_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n451\t    try:\n452\t        os.chmod(bridge_dir, 0o700)                          # owner-only, like the spine/keys dirs\n453\t    except OSError:\n454\t        pass\n455\t    tag = addr.replace(\":\", \"_\").replace(\"/\", \"_\").replace(\"%\", \"_\")   # one stable cert per bind addr\n456\t    certfile = bridge_dir / f\"cert-{tag}.pem\"\n457\t    keyfile = bridge_dir / f\"key-{tag}.pem\"\n458\t\n459\t    if certfile.is_file() and keyfile.is_file():             # REUSE → stable fingerprint (pin once)\n460\t        cert = x509.load_pem_x509_certificate(certfile.read_bytes())\n461\t        return str(certfile), str(keyfile), _fingerprint(cert)\n462\t\n463\t    key = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())\n464\t    name = x509.Name([x509.NameAttribute(NameOID.COMMON_NAME, addr)])\n465\t    try:                                                     # bind addr is an IP by construction (bind_ok)\n466\t        san = x509.SubjectAlternativeName([x509.IPAddress(_ip.ip_address(addr))])\n467\t    except ValueError:                                       # defensive: a hostname would use a DNS SAN\n468\t        san = x509.SubjectAlternativeName([x509.DNSName(addr)])\n469\t    now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)       # real wallclock (tz-aware; TLS validity, not spine math)\n470\t    cert = (\n471\t        x509.CertificateBuilder()\n472\t        .subject_name(name)\n473\t        .issuer_name(name)                                   # self-signed: issuer == subject\n474\t        .public_key(key.public_key())\n475\t        .serial_number(x509.random_serial_number())\n476\t        .not_valid_before(now - datetime.timedelta(minutes=5))   # small skew tolerance\n477\t        .not_valid_after(now + datetime.timedelta(days=730))     # ~2 years\n478\t        .add_extension(san, critical=False)\n479\t        .add_extension(x509.BasicConstraints(ca=False, path_length=None), critical=True)\n480\t        .sign(key, hashes.SHA256())\n481\t    )\n482\t    _write_secure(certfile, cert.public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM))\n483\t    _write_secure(keyfile, key.private_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM,\n484\t                                             serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8,\n485\t                                             serialization.NoEncryption()))\n486\t    return str(certfile), str(keyfile), _fingerprint(cert)\n487\t\n488\t\n489\tdef serve(*, addr: str, port: int = _DEFAULT_PORT, spine_path=None, tls: bool = True) -> None:\n490\t    \"\"\"Run the WireGuard-bound bridge transport. With `tls=True` (default) it wraps the listening\n491\t    socket in an owner-pinned self-signed TLS cert (`ensure_bridge_cert`) BEFORE serving, printing the\n492\t    `https://` bind URL + the sha256 fingerprint to pin on the phone. With `tls=False` it serves plain\n493\t    HTTP (degraded: no secure context → the PWA cannot install / register a service worker) and prints\n494\t    an honest warning. `build_server` stays plain — TLS lives only here.\"\"\"\n495\t    srv = build_server(addr=addr, port=port, spine_path=spine_path)\n496\t    bound = srv.server_address[1]\n497\t    if tls:\n498\t        import ssl\n499\t        certfile, keyfile, fp = ensure_bridge_cert(addr)\n500\t        ctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_SERVER)\n501\t        ctx.load_cert_chain(certfile, keyfile)\n502\t        srv.socket = ctx.wrap_socket(srv.socket, server_side=True)   # wrap the listener before serve_forever\n503\t        print(f\"  SIGIL bridge → https://{addr}:{bound}/   (WireGuard-bound; loopback/private only — NO wire secret)\")\n504\t        print(f\"  TLS fingerprint (sha256): {fp}\")\n505\t        print(\"    ↳ PIN this once on the phone — it is stable across restarts; a later change means MITM\")\n506\t    else:\n507\t        print(f\"  SIGIL bridge → http://{addr}:{bound}/   (WireGuard-bound; loopback/private only — NO wire secret)\")\n508\t        print(\"  WARNING: --no-tls → plain HTTP, NO secure context: the phone CANNOT install the PWA or\")\n509\t        print(\"           register a service worker. Use TLS unless you terminate it upstream in the tunnel.\")\n510\t    print(\"  pair a phone (owner, at the desktop):  sigil mesh authorize <device-id> <device-pubkey>\")\n511\t    print(\"  every request must carry an authorized-device signature (X-SIGIL-Envelope) — there is no token\")\n512\t    try:\n513\t        srv.serve_forever()\n514\t    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n515\t        srv.shutdown()\n516\t"}]},"uuid":"837235b4-7b1f-4e5a-9237-3ddf2043e744","timestamp":"2026-07-19T05:47:24.344Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7441132f-0549-47aa-be32-e8f2e8049364","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

(B) NEW inf-nonce -> RemoteDisconnected.\"\"\"\nimport sys, os, base64, json, threading, http.client, tempfile\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))\nfrom _h import OWNER, OP\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.mesh import authorize_device\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\nfrom sigil.bridge.server import build_server\nfrom sigil.bridge.envelope import build_core, sign_envelope\nfrom sigil.reuse import canonical_json\n\nCLK = 1700000000.0\nspine_path = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\ns = SpineStore(spine_path)\ndev = generate_keypair()\nauthorize_device(s, \"phone-1\", dev.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n\nsrv = build_server(addr=\"127.0.0.1\", port=0, spine_path=spine_path, trusted_pubkey=OP, clock=lambda: CLK)\nport = srv.server_address[1]\nt = threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True); t.start()\n\ndef env_header(payload):\n    raw = canonical_json(payload)\n    if isinstance(raw, str): raw = raw.encode()\n    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(raw).decode().rstrip(\"=\")\n\ndef get(path, payload):\n    c = http.client.HTTPConnection(\"127.0.0.1\", port, timeout=5)\n    c.request(\"GET\", path, headers={\"X-SIGIL-Envelope\": env_header(payload), \"Host\": f\"127.0.0.1:{port}\"})\n    r = c.getresponse(); body = r.read().decode(); c.close(); return r.status, body\n\ndef post(path, payload):\n    c = http.client.HTTPConnection(\"127.0.0.1\", port, timeout=5)\n    body = json.dumps({}).encode()\n    c.request(\"POST\", path, body=body,\n              headers={\"X-SIGIL-Envelope\": env_header(payload), \"Host\": f\"127.0.0.1:{port}\",\n                       \"Content-Type\":\"application/json\", \"Content-Length\": str(len(body))})\n    r = c.getresponse(); rb = r.read().decode(); c.close(); return r.status, rb\n\n# (A) BLOCK-3: malformed short sig on a read -> clean 401 (not a crash / RemoteDisconnected)\ncore = build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"read:pending\", {}, 1, CLK)\nbad = {**core, \"sig\": \"AAAA\"}\ntry:\n    st, b = get(\"/api/pending\", bad)\n    print(f\"[BLOCK-3] malformed sig read -> HTTP {st}  {b[:80]}\")\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f\"[BLOCK-3] malformed sig read -> CRASH {type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n\n# valid read works\ngood = sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"read:pending\", {}, 2, CLK))\nst, b = get(\"/api/pending\", good)\nprint(f\"[control] valid read -> HTTP {st}  {b[:80]}\")\n\n# (B) NEW: effectful panic with nonce=Infinity -> uncaught OverflowError -> connection dropped\ninf_env = sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"panic\", {}, float(\"inf\"), CLK))\ntry:\n    st, b = post(\"/api/panic\", inf_env)\n    print(f\"[NEW inf-nonce] panic(nonce=Infinity) -> HTTP {st}  {b[:80]}\")\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f\"[NEW inf-nonce] panic(nonce=Infinity) -> {type(e).__name__}: {e}  <<< no clean HTTP response (server thread crashed)\")\n\n# contrast: replayed effectful nonce -> clean 409\nr1 = sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"panic\", {}, 5, CLK))\nst, b = post(\"/api/panic\", r1); print(f\"[control] panic nonce=5 (first) -> HTTP {st} {b[:60]}\")\nst, b = post(\"/api/panic\", r1); print(f\"[control] panic nonce=5 (replay) -> HTTP {st} {b[:80]}\")\n\nsrv.shutdown()\nPY\ncd /home/kali/sigil && timeout 90 /home/kali/.sigil/venv/bin/python /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro_live.py 2>/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/live_stderr.txt; {"parentUuid":"9d5434b7-5626-4cc2-b01f-c1cba243e0fe","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a09f20522ff9ad768","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAtkmSYn9CZACBXWjeQd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01KEMRZXRiKBVAFQJzTUgQgM","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro_toctou.py <<'PY'\n\"\"\"Item 4: BLOCK-4 nonce TOCTOU — 8 concurrent replays of ONE effectful envelope -> exactly 1 accepted.\"\"\"\nimport sys, os, threading, tempfile\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))\nfrom _h import OWNER, OP\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.mesh import authorize_device, authorized_devices\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\nfrom sigil.bridge.envelope import consume, build_core, sign_envelope\n\npath = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\ns = SpineStore(path)\ndev = generate_keypair()\nauthorize_device(s, \"phone-1\", dev.public_key_b64, OWNER)\nauth = authorized_devices(s, OP)\nenv = sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"relay\", {\"text\":\"x\"}, 5, 1700000000))\n\nresults = []; barrier = threading.Barrier(8); lk = threading.Lock()\ndef worker():\n    barrier.wait()\n    fresh = SpineStore(path)   # mirror the server: fresh SpineStore per request\n    try:\n        consume(fresh, env, auth, effectful=True); r=\"ok\"\n    except ValueError: r=\"refused\"\n    with lk: results.append(r)\nts=[threading.Thread(target=worker) for _ in range(8)]\n[t.start() for t in ts]; [t.join() for t in ts]\nprint(\"accepted:\", results.count(\"ok\"), \"refused:\", results.count(\"refused\"))\nprint(\"STATUS:\", \"HELD (exactly 1)\" if results.count(\"ok\")==1 else \"BROKEN\")\nPY\ncat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro_stress.py <<'PY'\n\"\"\"Item 5 + DEADLOCK HUNT: heavy mixed concurrency (append + consume + submit_arm + submit_approval + read/verify).\nFresh SpineStore per op (like the server). Assert: completes (no hang), chain verifies, seqs distinct & contiguous, no reader crash.\"\"\"\nimport sys, os, threading, tempfile, random, time\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))\nfrom _h import OWNER, OP\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.mesh import authorize_device, authorized_devices\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair, sign, canonical_json\nfrom sigil.bridge.envelope import consume, build_core, sign_envelope\nfrom sigil.bridge.daemon import BridgeDaemon\nfrom sigil.gesture.session import sign_arm_request\nfrom sigil.agents.approvals import _approval_message, SIGNAL as APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n\npath = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\nboot = SpineStore(path)\n# authorize N devices\ndevs = []\nfor i in range(6):\n    d = generate_keypair(); authorize_device(boot, f\"dev{i}\", d.public_key_b64, OWNER); devs.append(d)\nauth = authorized_devices(boot, OP)\n\nN_THREADS = 24\nK = 40\nerrors = []; errlk = threading.Lock()\ndef rec_err(where, e):\n    with errlk: errors.append((where, type(e).__name__, str(e)[:100]))\n\ndef approval_payload(dev, tgt):\n    dec=\"approved\"; approver=\"dev\"\n    msg=_approval_message(tgt, dec, approver)\n    return {\"signal\":APPROVAL_SIGNAL,\"approval\":dec,\"target_seq\":tgt,\"approver\":approver,\n            \"pubkey\":dev.public_key_b64,\"sig\":sign(dev.private_key_b64,msg)}\n\ndef worker(tid):\n    dev = devs[tid % len(devs)]\n    nonce = tid*100000  # unique nonce space per thread's device-effectful ops (avoid cross-thread collision)\n    for k in range(K):\n        op = random.random()\n        try:\n            if op < 0.35:\n                SpineStore(path).append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"X\", payload={\"tid\":tid,\"k\":k})\n            elif op < 0.55:\n                nonce += 1\n                env = sign_envelope(dev, build_core(dev.public_key_b64, \"relay\", {\"text\":\"x\"}, nonce, 1700000000+nonce))\n                try: consume(SpineStore(path), env, auth, effectful=True)\n                except ValueError: pass\n            elif op < 0.72:\n                req = sign_arm_request(dev, device_id=f\"dev{tid%len(devs)}\", nonce=tid*1000+k, ts=time.time(), ttl_seconds=120.0)\n                try: BridgeDaemon(SpineStore(path), trusted_pubkey=OP).submit_arm_request(req)\n                except ValueError: pass\n            elif op < 0.85:\n                try: BridgeDaemon(SpineStore(path), trusted_pubkey=OP).submit_device_approval(approval_payload(dev, tid*10+k))\n                except ValueError: pass\n            else:\n                # concurrent READER + verify (no lock held) — stress read-during-write\n                st = SpineStore(path); Measure worst-case injection-after-panic window; release un-blocks <=TTL.\nUses the explicit-now API (_killswitch_engaged(now)) for determinism; and the arm path checks FRESH.\"\"\"\nimport sys, os\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))\nfrom _h import store, authed_device, OP, OWNER\nfrom sigil.gesture.session import SessionGate, _KS_CACHE_TTL\nfrom sigil.gesture.components import RecordingInputBackend\nfrom sigil.bridge.daemon import BridgeDaemon\nfrom sigil.governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\nfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\n\nclass Cls:\n    def classify(self, tool): return Tier.A1\nprint(\"_KS_CACHE_TTL =\", _KS_CACHE_TTL)\n\n# DANGEROUS direction: panic honored — measure the injection-after-panic window\ns = store(); dev,_ = authed_device(s)\ng = SessionGate(s, RecordingInputBackend(), classifier=Cls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\ng._killswitch_engaged(1000.0)                      # prime cache: not engaged, checked at t=1000\nBridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).panic_engage(by=\"test\")   # PANIC now\nw = {}\nfor dt in (0.0, 0.1, 0.4, 0.49, 0.5, 0.51, 1.0):\n    w[dt] = g._killswitch_engaged(1000.0+dt)       # True => halt seen\nfirst_true = next((dt for dt in sorted(w) if w[dt]), None)\nprint(\"after panic, _killswitch_engaged at t+dt:\", {k: w[k] for k in sorted(w)})\nprint(f\"  worst-case injection-after-panic window = {first_true}s (<= TTL {_KS_CACHE_TTL})\")\n\n# arm path checks FRESH (uncached): a panic'd spine cannot arm at all\ns2 = store();
